- **Path to playable — Phase A (season spine) COMPLETE; PM-2-sim REACHED July 27, 2026. A1–A5 + A4b all LANDED; A4a remains gated on match realism, not on compute** — *opened July 25, 2026; Phase A closed July 27, 2026.* `docs/tracking/path-to-playable-roadmap.md` (v0.4, ROADMAP — meta-planning, opens no numbered spec) sequences already-approved specs and converged design supplements into the shortest path to a build a person can play, against the PM-1 (playable match) / **PM-2 (playable season — the objective)** / PM-3 (playable career) ladder. It splits the work on one line — **Track S** (simulation/loop: #30, #37, #44, the league bootstrap; host-free, gated by the Linux `tools/dotnet-ci` shim on every push, **no external blocker**) and **Track C** (client/presentation; Unity P4–P6 are host-gated, which the B6 decision routes around by extending the existing `LiveMatchServer` browser surface first). Its five quantified constraints drive the ordering: **C1** full-fidelity season simulation is infeasible (≥ 154 s of compute per match at the certified p50, ≥ 16.3 h per 380-match season — both *lower* bounds, since wall-clock tracks the mean and p99 = 2.5669 ms), so the #30 KD-9 round-resolution model is on the critical path at a ≲ 10 ms/match budget, not an optimisation after it; **C1a** that model needs a ~200-match engine-simulated calibration corpus (~9 h, parallelisable, run-once) budgeted as its own item; **C2** the Unity host block and the browser fallback; **C3** `RosterGenerator` already produces deterministic 25-player club squads, so a thin `LeagueBootstrap` defers #47 (the DB editor) entirely past PM-3; **C5** every never-compiled spec carries defect latency (expect 1–3 ERR-class findings per T-phase landing). **Zero new numbered specs are required to reach PM-2** — three items need only a design note (§6). **A1 (#30 T0) LANDED July 25, 2026:** the season value types, fixture scheduler, league table, calendar, board, match-outcome payload, season state and view model, in the existing `TacticalDirector.SeasonSave` assembly (97 season-save tests); it surfaced **ERR-030-010** (§3.1's round-parity venue rule is authoritative; the §3.7 / Appendix C worked tables were hand-derived without it), and five adversarial-review passes then fixed 1H+5M+7L (headline: `SeasonState.Table` was a public mutable handle defeating the KD-7 single-writer contract; an aggregate-field-count coupling guard whose first fix was incomplete across five value types; the `[GT]` catalogue rows re-migrated onto `GameplayConfig`). **A2 (#30 T1) LANDED July 25, 2026:** the season save/restore path — a `SeasonStateCodec` over the #30 Appendix B layout, the save frame gaining a third opaque sub-blob, **`SEASON_SAVE_FORMAT_VERSION` 1 → 2** (world and match blobs byte-untouched, FR-SN-020), and `Save`/`Load` gaining the season (FR-SN-021); season-save tests 112 → 135, full gate green. C5's prediction held again: it surfaced **ERR-030-011** (§3.6's `EncodeSeason` pseudocode omitted `ManagedClubId`, which Appendix B row 3a requires — a codec written to §3.6 verbatim emits a blob no season can be reconstructed from; and Appendix B row 11's open `f32/u8` job security is now pinned to the integer per-mille the code carries), and a code self-review closed an encode/decode asymmetry in the T0 `SeasonState` constructor plus an overflowable length bound. See the root Last-Updated entry + src/CLAUDE.md v2.37. **A3 (league bootstrap) LANDED July 25, 2026** (season-save 135 → 168 tests, player-database 42 → 46, full gate green), closing §6 item 1's governance gap with the new converged supplement `docs/tracking/league-bootstrap-design.md` (v1.1; AR-1..AR-3 converged, AR-4 over the shipped code 2M+4L). `LeagueBootstrap.Generate(worldSeed, clubCount)` → `League`: N clubs × 25 **position-coherent** players from one seed via three domain-separated derivations, a seeded strength ramp so the table is not 20 identical teams, `League` **is** the `ISquadProvider`, and `CreateSeason` hands #30 a startable `SeasonState`. It allocates **nothing** in #16 (the strength permutation uses a local SplitMix64 like `FixtureScheduler`, leaving `DOMAIN_TAG_SEASON_LOOP`/ordinal 84 pinned to A4's first draw site per ERR-030-001). C5's prediction held in a new form — the H finding surfaced at **design** time rather than after landing: uniform position draws leave ~3% of squads unable to field a back four, which `LineupSelector` refuses fail-loud, so a league would have failed to start *by seed*; fixed with a `[GT]` position template + an additive byte-identical `RosterGenerator` overload (KD-6). AR-4 also recorded two **forward gaps for A4**: `LineupSelector` is `internal` to match-engine so the quick-sim's `Rating(club)` is unreachable from `SeasonLoop` (A4 must expose it, not re-implement selection), and A4a's harness belongs in `src/season-save/tests/`. **A4 (#30 T2) LANDED July 26, 2026** (season-save 179 → 240 tests (237 passed + the 3 env-gated calibration/diagnostic drivers skipped), incl. the `season-multi-fixture` capstone, full gate green): the `SeasonLoop` composition root with the KD-2 fixed day-advance order (only the world tick live), whole-round resolution routed by the new `RoundResolutionMode` dial, the keyed `RoundResolutionModel` quick-sim (a 380-fixture season in milliseconds against C1's ≥ 16 h), the `DOMAIN_TAG_SEASON_LOOP = 0x22` back-prop **at its first draw site**, and the capstone. C5's prediction held for the fourth consecutive landing, and this time it produced the finding that reshapes the plan: **ERR-030-012** (§4.5's registered cursor stream contradicts §3.4.1's keyed-draw requirement — `SubsystemOrdinals.SeasonLoop` deliberately not allocated, since an ordinal with no stream is the FR-LW-031 phantom), **ERR-030-013** (§4.6's `SeasonState`-resident outcome record is unimplementable), and **ERR-030-014** — running A4a's Step 0 pilot showed **a production match never puts the ball in motion**, so every engine match is 0–0 (see the match-engine-playability OPEN ISSUES entry immediately above). **A4b LANDED July 26, 2026** (the possession bootstrap — five seams, ERR-030-014 closed; a production match now plays). **A5 (#30 T3) LANDED July 27, 2026** (season-save 240 → 263 tests, full gate green): `SeasonLoop.RollToNextSeason()`, the KD-6 restartable boundary transform — finalize → board evaluate → (a') #43 / (b') #40 declared-and-empty insertion points → derive seed → regenerate schedule → rebuild calendar → (d) #28 empty → reset table. **Pure in the prior `SeasonState`** (no clock read, no draw), which is what makes FR-SN-029's restartability substantive rather than incidental; compute-and-validate before any write, with the throwing commit ahead of the non-throwing one, so a refused roll changes nothing. C5 held for the sixth consecutive landing — **ERR-030-015**: §3.5's pseudocode regenerates `Fixtures` but **never rebuilds `Calendar`**, so a season rolled from the spec as written is *permanently unplayable*, and — the part worth remembering — **no assertion over the rolled state's fields would have caught it**; it took an acceptance test that plays a second season to completion (9 of 18 predicates fail pre-fix). **An adversarial review over the landing then found 1H+3M+2L, all fixed** — headline: `AdvanceDays` bounded the clock only in-season, so walking the close season past the next season's opening day reached a career that could be neither played nor rolled, and that saved and reloaded cleanly; plus board policy duplicated onto the composition root, and a determinism test whose schedule assertion was structurally unreachable behind an always-true disjunction. **With A5, Phase A's exit `PM-2-sim` is REACHED**: a full 38-round season simulates head-lessly, saves and resumes byte-identically, and rolls into a second season, with two careers from one seed agreeing on both tables. **What remains on this track is quality, not structure.** **A4a is still gated, and no longer on compute** — Step 0 is ~33 min and the corpus ~1.4 h, both affordable, but the engine's goal rate runs **~4.7× football's**, so a corpus fitted today would calibrate the quick-sim to reproduce that across a whole 380-fixture league; Step 0 cannot catch it alone, since it only asks whether the strength extremes separate and it passed at 25–0. The recorded next lever is the **quality of the goalkeeper's save** (§5.Z.15) ahead of any further shot or finishing tuning. Track C (B1–B6) is independent and unblocked.

- **Snapshot deserialize / restore path — design supplement AR-CONVERGED; Phases 1 + 2 LANDED (writer + reader + factory + G3 round-trip determinism + distinct-squad re-projection); Phase 3 on-disk `SaveManager` fold LANDED (July 21, 2026); native MXCSR live-mode query CERTIFIED July 22, 2026; N2 unified season save LANDED July 22, 2026 — Phase 3 COMPLETE** — *opened July 20, 2026; Phases 1 + 2 complete July 20, 2026; Phase 3 on-disk fold July 21, 2026; N2 season save July 22, 2026.* `MatchEngine.SerializeWorldState` writes the full cross-tick world-state field set but **nothing reads it back** — verified: no `Read`/`Deserialize`/`RestoreFromSnapshot` in `MatchEngine.cs`. This is the keystone the next tier of MVP work sits behind: distinct-squad restore fidelity (#27 T3, KD-T3-3), runtime MXCSR float-mode validation (#16 §4.8.2 — "no consumer yet"), save/load of an in-progress match, replay/rewind, and (upstream of career mode) the unified season save. `docs/tracking/snapshot-deserialize-design.md` (v0.5, DESIGN SUPPLEMENT — same governance class as `match-engine-design.md`) designs that reader: KD-1 one symmetric version-gated fail-loud `DeserializeWorldState` (schema read first, trailing-byte guard); KD-2 reconstruct through `RestoreState` seams, not raw pokes; KD-3 distinct-squad re-projection via a factory-owned `ISquadProvider` keyed by the serialized `_activeBenchSlot` (fail-loud when a non-sentinel `_rosterClubId` has no provider); KD-4 a static `RestoreFromSnapshot(in SnapshotHeader header, SnapshotPayload payload, …, ISquadProvider squads = null)` factory (validate fingerprint → boot → `EventBus.ResetForNewMatch` → deserialize + re-project → restore digest chain + clock); KD-5 digest-chain continuity via `SnapshotCodec.CommitLoadedDigest` + `MatchClock.RestoreFromSnapshot`, with **round-trip determinism (save@N → restore → tick to N+K == uninterrupted run)** as the correctness contract; KD-6 the MXCSR/`EnvironmentFingerprint` gate wires at restore step 0 (native query stays host-blocked); KD-8 the writer's one genuine gap. The AR cycle converged over four rounds — AR-1 (3M+2L structural), AR-2 (1M, the header/payload contract gap), **AR-3 (1H — the RNG-stream serialization hole)**, AR-4 (1L, convergence). **Phase 1 is phased into slices; the KD-8 writer half LANDED July 20, 2026:** the `match-flow.card-severity` `RngStreamState` cursor (RngCursor + ActionOrdinal — the engine's ONLY mutable RNG stream; collision self-seeds `matchSeed ^ frameNumber` and pass/shot error is hash-based, both tick-reconstructible) is now serialized at **`SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` 16 → 17** (`MatchEngine.cs` v1.40 / `MatchEngineConstants.cs` v1.24), so a save taken after any booking round-trips deterministically — before v17 a restore re-registered the stream at cursor 0 and the next card draw diverged, silently breaking the KD-5 contract for any carded match. The stale v8 "no cross-tick state excluded" exclusion-proof note is corrected. New `TestOnly_SetCardSeverityStreamCursor` seam + `MatchEngineSnapshotSchemaTests` v1.14 (pin 17 + `CardSeverityRngCursor_FeedsSnapshotDigest` probe). **The reader half LANDED July 20, 2026 (`MatchEngine.cs` v1.41 — Phase 1 complete):** `DeserializeWorldState` (the symmetric line-for-line mirror of `SerializeWorldState` + per-block `Read*` helpers, reconstructing subsystem state through each `RestoreState` seam — KD-1/KD-2); the new `RestoreState` counterparts on Pressing/Defensive/Attacking (`RestoreState(in XxxTickState)`), Perception (`RestoreState(in PerceptionTickState)`), Positioning (`RestoreState(HysteresisState)`) + `MovementCommand.ReconstructFromSnapshot` (RotationController's `Restore*` seams already existed); and the static `RestoreFromSnapshot(in SnapshotHeader, SnapshotPayload, ulong matchSeed)` factory (KD-4/KD-6: fingerprint gate step 0 against `CreateStage0Dev` → boot + `EventBus.ResetForNewMatch` → deserialize → KD-3 distinct-squad fail-loud → `SnapshotCodec.CommitLoadedDigest` + clock restore, KD-5). New `MatchEngineSnapshotRestoreTests` (G3 round-trip: neutral kickoff / mid-match tactics changed / the KD-8 booking-cursor regression, plus version-gate + trailing-byte + distinct-squad fail-loud). No `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` change. **Two implementation findings folded in (both verified against source):** (1) the excluded `_possessingAgentId`/`_prevPossessingAgentId` (folded into `MatchContext.PossessingAgentId` per the writer's exclusion proof) are read at the next tick's start, so the reader reconstructs both from the restored MatchContext — the `_prev == _poss == MatchContext.PossessingAgentId` snapshot-time invariant makes one field sufficient; without it any match that developed possession (a first-touch control) diverged; (2) the KD-1 exact-`BytesWritten` trailing guard was too strict — `RunSnapshotPhase` appends the digest-load-bearing event ledger (`EventBus.SerializeLedger`, 1-byte domain tag + u32 count + Tier A records) after the world state, so the reader does not restore the ledger (replayed forward; baked into the header digest the factory inherits) but validates the world-state read ended exactly at the ledger domain-tag boundary (the R1 drift check) and byte-exact-accounts the empty-ledger common case. **Full dotnet gate: PASSED, 0 failures (257 match-engine tests; whole tree green — SDK installed via apt).** **Phase 2 LANDED July 20, 2026 (`MatchEngine.cs` v1.42) — #27 T3 distinct-squad re-projection, which closes the last T3 data-side item.** New `ISquadProvider` seam (`src/match-engine/ISquadProvider.cs`) threaded into `RestoreFromSnapshot(…, ISquadProvider squads = null)`; `ReprojectDistinctSquads` replaces the Phase-1 fail-loud — neutral fast-path returns immediately, and each team with a non-sentinel `_rosterClubId` has its roster resolved (ClubId-check + size/record validation, both teams before any apply — the `ConfigureSquads` validate-both-before-write discipline), its base lineup re-projected via `LineupSelector` + `PlayerAttributeProjection` (`ReprojectBaseLineup`, attribute arrays only — plus the bench GK flags `_benchIsGoalkeeper`, a boot-constant NOT serialized; the on-pitch `_isGoalkeeper` stays the restored serialized value), and the substitutions the serialized `_activeBenchSlot` records replayed (`ReprojectSubstitutions`, the attribute half of `SubstitutePlayer`). Fail-loud on absent provider / unresolvable ClubId / mismatched returned ClubId (R4). Determinism rests on the provider returning the SAME roster the saved match loaded (`LineupSelector` + `PlayerAttributeProjection` are pure). `MatchEngineSnapshotRestoreTests` v1.1: G3 round-trip for a distinct (varied-attribute) squad, a mid-match substitution, a post-restore substitution (bench-attr fidelity), and a post-restore keeper-for-keeper substitution (bench-GK-flag re-projection), plus fail-loud on no provider / unknown ClubId / mismatched roster. No `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` change. **Full dotnet gate: PASSED, 0 failures (263 match-engine tests; whole tree green).** **Discovered during Phase 2 (out of scope; recorded as a Phase-1 snapshot-completeness follow-up, NOT a Phase-2 defect):** a post-restore substitution that FLIPS a pitch slot's goalkeeper status — subbing a keeper onto an OUTFIELD slot, which realistic play never does — diverges, traced to the Positioning AI (#12) producing a different formation slot for that agent after restore (an un-captured interaction of the GK-flag flip with the Positioning/rotation state; two fresh engines with the same substitution are deterministic, and the base distinct-squad round-trip + realistic keeper-for-keeper and outfielder substitutions all round-trip). **RESOLVED July 22, 2026:** the divergence was `SlotComposer.Compose` (`src/positioning-ai/SlotComposer.cs` v1.3) skipping (`continue`) every GK-flagged agent — the GK is composed once into `outSlots[0]`, so a GK flag on a non-zero slot left that slot's composed position frozen at the stale value carried in the un-serialized positioning slot buffer from before the flip; a restored engine boot-seeds that buffer differently and never recomputes the skipped slot, diverging its `GetFormationSlot`/`BaselineSlot` and cascading into the digest chain. The fix gives a GK-flagged non-zero slot the stateless GK slot formula (a pure function of ball position) instead of skipping it, so the slot is reproducible from the serialized `_isGoalkeeper` flag alone. A GK at slot 0 (every realistic case, incl. keeper-for-keeper subs) never takes the new branch ⇒ byte-identical default pipeline; no `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` change. Locked by `MatchEngineSnapshotRestoreTests.RoundTrip_KeeperSubstitutedOntoOutfieldSlot_IsDeterministic` (fails pre-fix, passes post-fix). **Phase 3 on-disk `SaveManager` fold (N1) LANDED July 21, 2026 (`MatchEngine.cs` v1.43)** — the on-disk save-file format that *calls* the reader, governed by the new converged supplement `docs/tracking/match-save-file-design.md` (v0.3, AR-1 3M+2L / AR-2 CONVERGED). New `src/match-engine/` `MatchSaveManager` (atomic `Save(engine, path)` / `Load(path, ISquadProvider squads = null) → MatchEngine`, the §4.6.1.1 temp→fsync→rename contract mirrored — `SaveManager` exposes no raw-blob write, so it is re-implemented not delegated) + the pure `MatchSaveCodec` (a single `MATCH_SAVE_FORMAT_VERSION`-gated blob = the KD-7 boot-`matchSeed` boot-header + the `SnapshotHeader` incl. its `EnvironmentFingerprint` + digest chain + the `SnapshotPayload`; fail-loud on version mismatch / an out-of-bounds length prefix / trailing bytes, the `WorldStateSerializer.ReadCount` posture, with an overflow-safe bound guard) + `MatchSaveContents`. `MatchEngine` gains a public `MatchSeed` property (the boot seed the payload does not carry — KD-2/KD-7) and the two durable-capture seams promoted `TestOnly_` → production internal (`CaptureDurableHeader`/`CaptureDurablePayload`, now consumed by `MatchSaveManager`; the restore tests repointed to the production names). `MatchEngineConstants.MATCH_SAVE_FORMAT_VERSION` [FIXED] = 1 — a THIRD version distinct from the two snapshot schema versions it frames. **Decisions made at this N1 root:** (a) the file carries the boot `matchSeed` (the KD-7 O1(b) boot-header, now decided — the file is self-sufficient); (b) the `EnvironmentFingerprint` is serialized so the KD-6 gate runs **end-to-end through disk** (a save under `CreateStage0Dev` validates on load; a tampered/foreign fingerprint is rejected) — this closes O3 for the on-disk path, so the on-disk header no longer writes `Fingerprint = null` the way the deterministic-sim `SaveManager` does (the specific N1 gap); (c) the `ISquadProvider` is a Load-time parameter, never persisted (the file references rosters by ClubId; the caller owns the roster store). New `MatchSaveManagerTests` (16 — disk round-trip determinism for neutral / booking-before-save / distinct-squad-via-provider, `MatchSaveCodec` round-trip + all fail-loud gates, and `MatchSaveManager` missing-file / corrupt-file / no-provider / overwrite paths). Code self-adversarial review: 1 hardening (the `Require` bound guard rewritten overflow-safe — a corrupt near-`int.MaxValue` length prefix could wrap `offset + need` negative and slip past; now compares against `total − offset`), locked by `Codec_OversizePayloadLength_FailsLoud`. No `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` change (a file frame around the unchanged reader/writer). **Full dotnet gate: PASSED, 0 failures (279 match-engine tests; whole tree green — SDK installed via apt).** **Still open:** the native MXCSR live-mode query — the *code* LANDED July 21, 2026 (the §4.8.2 gate is built and wired): new `src/deterministic-sim/native/mxcsr_query.c` (the `td_get_mxcsr()` = `_mm_getcsr()`/STMXCSR shim), `MxcsrNative.cs` (`[DllImport("td_mxcsr")]` + `TryQuery`, absent-library ⇒ probe-unavailable no-op), and `MxcsrValidator.cs` (decode DAZ/FTZ/RC + `ValidateStage0FloatMode()` fail-loud on divergence), called at `MatchEngine` boot AND `RestoreFromSnapshot` step 0. Full dotnet gate PASSED (whole tree green; 279 match-engine + the new `MxcsrValidatorTests` pure-decode locks). It is a no-op on the Linux CI gate / any host without the compiled plugin, and enforces only where the native library loads (the pinned cert host) — defense-in-depth over the certified pin, NOT a replacement for the determinism-KAT proof. **Host-block CLEARED July 22, 2026:** the compiled `td_mxcsr` plugin (built MSVC `cl /O2 /LD` on the pinned Windows 11 / Unity 6000.4.9f1 / DX11 / Mono / x64 / SSE4.2 host) + the certified live read landed — live MXCSR `0x1FBF`, mode fields DAZ=off / FTZ=off / RC=nearest-even all match the Stage-0 pin (the low 6 bits are sticky exception STATUS flags the pin correctly ignores, per the `ExceptionFlagsSet_DoNotAffectPin` lock); `ValidateStage0FloatMode()` returned `Validated`. Evidence: `docs/specs/deterministic-sim/cert-runs/mxcsr-live-mode-cert-2026-07-22.md`; plugin committed at `src/deterministic-sim/native/td_mxcsr.dll` (+ Unity `.meta`; see `src/deterministic-sim/native/README.md`). The §4.8.2 gate is now ENFORCED (not just built) on the certified host. **The N2 unified match/season save LANDED July 22, 2026** — the new season save-file root `TacticalDirector.SeasonSave` (`src/season-save/`; governed by the new converged `docs/tracking/unified-season-save-design.md` v0.4, AR-1 2M+3L → AR-2 CONVERGENCE, code self-AR clean) sits **above both** `match-engine` and `living-world` (the only assembly that may reference both — resolving the FR-LW-003 blocker, since neither of those may reference the other) and writes ONE file bundling the `WorldStore.Snapshot()` composite + an **optional** in-progress match blob as two opaque, independently version-gated sub-blobs (a `matchPresent` flag byte for the no-match-between-fixtures case). `SeasonSaveCodec` never parses either sub-blob, so all four inner versions stay untouched — the season file only adds a fourth `SEASON_SAVE_FORMAT_VERSION` [FIXED] = 1. `MatchSaveManager` gained public `Encode(engine) → byte[]` / `Restore(blob, squads) → MatchEngine` (the "match save as a value" API the root composes without the internal capture seams; `Save`/`Load` refactored to delegate, all 279 match-engine tests still green). New `SeasonSaveManagerTests` (19 — disk round-trip determinism for a no-match season + a season with a neutral / distinct-squad match via `ISquadProvider`, codec round-trip + all fail-loud gates, manager fail-loud paths incl. the code-AR R4 no-match-with-provider lock). No `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` / `WORLD_STORE_FORMAT_VERSION` / `MATCH_SAVE_FORMAT_VERSION` change. A follow-up code AR over the shipped diff fixed 2 L (Save guard-order restored behaviour-identical; the R4 test lock). Full dotnet gate PASSED (whole tree green; 19 new season-save tests; SDK via apt). **With this, snapshot-deserialize Phase 3 is complete** — and the Phase-1 Positioning GK-flag-flip snapshot-completeness edge above was RESOLVED July 22, 2026 (see that paragraph), so nothing further is tracked open on this line. Promoted into `match-engine-design.md` as Phase G.

- **`EnvironmentFingerprint.floatModelHash` — hasher + §4.8.3 Mono mapping LANDED (Option A); §4.8.2 runtime MXCSR gate code LANDED (July 21, 2026); compiled plugin + certified live read LANDED July 22, 2026 (ERR-016-006)** — *opened July 19, 2026; Option A landed same day; MXCSR gate July 21, 2026.* The `SessionManifest` requires an `EnvironmentFingerprint` (#16 §4.8), whose `floatModelHash` (§4.8.3) is `SHA-256(SerializeCanonical(0x14 ‖ floatFlagTuple))` over an 11-field compiler/runtime float-mode tuple. **Original gap:** no code computed that hash for a live host (the field was a plain constructor string; the only factory, `CreateStage0Dev()`, stamped a `STAGE0_DEV_PLACEHOLDER` sentinel — `ComputeDigest()` hashes the *outer* 6-field fingerprint, a different digest), and the tuple's fields 1–4 (`compilerToolchain`/`compilerVersion`/`targetTriple`/`il2cppVersion`) were IL2CPP-shaped while §5.5 row 0 / §4.8.3 field 4 required IL2CPP and rejected `"MONO"` — contradicting the **Mono** Stage-0 backend pinned in `certification-platform.md` v1.3 (the spec text May 3–4 predates the June-7 pin). **Resolved via Option A (owner sign-off July 19, 2026; `docs/tracking/env-fingerprint-float-model-hash-mono-mapping.md` v0.2 §7):** the §4.8.3 tuple is mapped onto the Mono backend, keeping the 11-field shape. **Spec (`section-4.md` v1.1 / `section-5.md` v1.1):** field 1 gains `"Mono"`; field 4 flips so Stage-0 certification ACCEPTS `"MONO"` (reject-MONO / IL2CPP-required → Stage 5+); a "Stage-0 Mono backend mapping" paragraph pins fields 1–4 (toolchain `"Mono"`, compilerVersion = host-supplied Mono version, targetTriple RID `"win-x64"`, il2cpp `"MONO"`); §5.5 row 0 backend → Mono + §5.5.1 Mono note. **Code:** new `src/deterministic-sim/FloatFlagTuple.cs` (`ComputeHash()` = the live-host hasher) + `EnvironmentFingerprint.CreateStage0MonoCertified(monoRuntimeVersion)` (v1.3) — a genuine, non-placeholder fingerprint from the Option-A fields + the §4.8.3 Required Stage-0 flag values; `CreateStage0Dev()` fixed `"SSE2"` → `"SSE4.2"` (v1.2) + named `FloatModelHashDevPlaceholder` sentinel + `IsDevPlaceholder` gate. Golden vector (`89f50a31…f343e7`, Python-mirror-verified) + determinism/sensitivity tests in `DeterministicSimTests`. **`monoRuntimeVersion` (field 2) is host-supplied — deliberately not synthesised.** **UPDATE July 19, 2026 — (b) the full platform-determinism certification is now DONE:** the determinism-KAT run executed on the pinned Windows 11 / Unity 6000.4.9f1 / DX11 / Mono host (commit `819f9d1`, `TacticalDirector.DeterministicSim.Tests` under Unity Test Framework EditMode) — all three golden-vector corpora (#16 §9.5 #4 a/b/c) + the §5 determinism-tier locks pass byte-exact (44 passed / 0 failed / 4 Stage-0+1-deferred skips); `certification-platform.md` v1.4 → **✅ PINNED**, `FR-DS-009-GATE` closed. Run record + raw NUnit evidence: `docs/specs/deterministic-sim/cert-runs/determinism-cert-2026-07-19.md` (+ `determinism-results-2026-07-19.xml`). **Remaining host-blocked / consumer-blocked:** (a) the §4.8.2 **runtime MXCSR validation** — the *code* LANDED July 21, 2026 (`src/deterministic-sim/native/mxcsr_query.c` STMXCSR shim + `MxcsrNative.cs` P/Invoke + `MxcsrValidator.cs` decode/gate, called at `MatchEngine` boot AND `RestoreFromSnapshot` step 0 — the snapshot-deserialize/restore path that gives it its consumer now exists; full dotnet gate PASSED). It *enforces* the now-certified pin as defense-in-depth (a no-op off the pinned host / without the compiled plugin), and is not part of *proving* the bits exact (the KAT run is that proof). The compiled `td_mxcsr` plugin build + the certified live golden read on the pinned host LANDED July 22, 2026 (live MXCSR `0x1FBF`; mode fields DAZ/FTZ/RC match the Stage-0 pin; `ValidateStage0FloatMode()` → `Validated`) — evidence `docs/specs/deterministic-sim/cert-runs/mxcsr-live-mode-cert-2026-07-22.md`, plugin `src/deterministic-sim/native/td_mxcsr.dll` (see `src/deterministic-sim/native/README.md`). No host-block remains on the MXCSR gate. **UPDATE July 19, 2026 — the FR-PO-052 perf-baseline certified capture is DONE:** the pinned-host 100-run capture (p50=0.4768 / p99=2.5669 ms/tick) was promoted PENDING → CERTIFIED (`docs/specs/performance-optimization/baselines/match-engine/kickoff-multi-second.cert.md` v1.2; code entry `CertifiedPerfBaselineTests.KickoffCertified()` swapped `Pending()` → `Certified()`), using `CreateStage0MonoCertified("mono-bundled-unity6000.4.9f1")` for the host-supplied Mono version (§4.8.3 field 2 — Unity versions its forked Mono runtime by editor release) ⇒ `floatModelHash 73c47ad5…b6bb756a` (golden-vector-validated Python mirror); Platform Certification owner sign-off recorded via the PR merge. The recorded tuple uses the pinned Stage-0 flag values, which is exactly what (a) validates against. **Latent, not live:** `SaveManager` still writes the header `Fingerprint = null`; the fingerprint is load-bearing only at a real cert run. - **Goalkeeper Mechanics (#11) / Heading Mechanics (#10) engine integration — Phase 1 (opt-in) LANDED; the GK/Heading attribute projections now have a live consumer** — *opened + Phase 1 landed July 22, 2026.* The `ToGoalkeeper`/`ToHeading` projections `player-attribute-projection-design.md` deferred under KD-P8 (as phantom consumers — `MatchEngine` built neither struct) are now non-phantom: #10/#11 are wired into the engine, and the projections seed the intents. Governed by the new converged supplement `docs/tracking/gk-heading-engine-integration-design.md` (AR-1 1M+2L → AR-2 CONVERGENCE → AR-3 scope revision to opt-in phasing; code self-AR folded CS0118 fully-qualification, `_gkAgentIds` refresh across `ConfigureSquads`/subs, and guard placement). **Phase 1 (this landing):** `MatchEngine.cs` v1.44 constructs both sealed orchestrators + four stateless ball/RNG adapters at boot and registers `heading.mechanics` + `goalkeeper.mechanics` RNG streams (the card-severity precedent, KD-1; `entityId: -1`, `SubsystemOrdinals.HeadingMechanics`=6 / `GoalkeeperMechanics`=7). A new public `EnableGkHeading()` opts in (KD-11, default off): while off the engine is **byte-identical to pre-wiring** (no `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` change — verified by the unchanged 279-test existing snapshot/determinism/restore suite); while on, `DriveGkHeadingTactical` (10 Hz, in `RunAiPhase`) + `DriveGkHeadingPhysics` (60 Hz, in `RunPhysicsPhase` before the Resolve goal check) drive both, and conservative Stage-0 world-state triggers (§4 — the `MatchFlowCollisionConsumer` heuristic-foul precedent) commit a `SaveIntent` seeded from `ToGoalkeeper` (loose on-target ball near the defended goal) and a `HeaderIntent` seeded from `ToHeading` (nearest agent to a loose airborne ball) — the projections' live consumer. The two RNG adapters draw from the single per-subsystem stream, accept-and-ignoring the draw-site id per the Stage-0 stub posture (KD-3). A flag-on engine is deterministic FORWARD but not yet snapshot-safe, so `CaptureDurableHeader`/`CaptureDurablePayload` **fail loud** (`NotSupportedException`) when on (KD-11 / §6) — the per-tick digest `SerializeWorldState` is untouched, so a flag-on engine still ticks. New `src/match-engine/PlayerAttributeProjection.cs` v1.2 (`ToGoalkeeper` int→float widen of the ten GK fields; `ToHeading` raw copy of Heading/Strength/Balance; runtime TeamId/Fatigue per KD-P4), `MatchEngineConstants.cs` v1.25 (+6 `[GT]` trigger constants). New `MatchEngineGkHeadingTests` (8 — flag semantics, flag-off default determinism + commits-nothing, save/header commit the projection, distinct-squad roster GK Pace flows through, flag-on forward determinism, durable-capture fails-loud-on / succeeds-off) + `PlayerAttributeProjectionTests` +2 (ToHeading/ToGoalkeeper field-scale). **Full dotnet gate: PASSED, 0 failures (whole tree green; 290 match-engine tests; SDK 8.0.129 via apt).** **Phase 2 cross-tick state serialization LANDED July 23, 2026 (`MatchEngine.cs` v1.46 — a flag-on engine is now snapshot-safe):** the GK (#11) / Heading (#10) cross-tick state is serialized at **`SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` 17 → 18**, so `EnableGkHeading()`-on save/restore round-trips deterministically and the Phase-1 `RequireGkHeadingSnapshotSafe` fail-loud guard (+ its two call sites) is removed. New typed value carriers `GoalkeeperTickState` (`src/goalkeeper-mechanics/`) + `HeadingTickState` (`src/heading-mechanics/`) with `CaptureState()`/`RestoreState(in …)` seams on both orchestrators (the `PressingTickState` Option-B convention — the orchestrator returns a typed carrier over its live arrays, `MatchEngine` owns 100% of the byte layout). The v18 block (`MatchEngine.WriteGoalkeeperState`/`ReadGoalkeeperState` + `WriteHeadingState`/`ReadHeadingState`, written UNCONDITIONALLY after the v17 card cursor) = the opt-in flag itself (so a restore reproduces the engine's mode) + the two subsystem RNG-stream cursors (`heading.mechanics` / `goalkeeper.mechanics`, RngCursor + ActionOrdinal, the card-severity precedent) + the two §4 trigger latches (`_saveCommittedForGk` / `_headerCommittedThisEpisode` — a completeness finding surfaced during implementation: engine-level cross-tick state that GATES trigger re-commits, so a restore that omitted them would re-fire a suppressed trigger and diverge) + both orchestrators' full in-flight arrays via the seams. Forward-determinism completeness proof: both orchestrators hold ONLY `readonly` deps + `readonly` arrays (no scalar mutable field), so the array inventory is provably the complete cross-tick surface; the excluded `_lastCommitted*Attrs` are write-only TestOnly observation and `_gkAgentIds` is rebuilt each drive (`RefreshGkAgentIds`). Flag-off is a deterministic no-op through the block (the version bump moves the digest; comparative round-trip contract, no absolute-golden rebaseline). New `MatchEngineSnapshotRestoreTests` flag-on round-trips (neutral + with a committed save advancing the GK RNG cursor / latches / `GkContactState` arrays — the omit-a-field-and-diverge completeness lock), `MatchEngineSnapshotSchemaTests` pin 17 → 18 + `GkHeadingState_FeedsSnapshotDigest` probe, `MatchEngineGkHeadingTests` durable-capture-succeeds (replacing the Phase-1 fails-loud lock). **Full dotnet gate: PASSED, 0 failures (whole tree green; 304 match-engine tests; SDK 8.0.129 via apt).** **The closed-loop `#19 ScenarioRunner` scenario LANDED July 23, 2026** (governed by the new converged supplement `docs/tracking/gk-heading-scenario-design.md` v1.0; AR-1 outline 3M+2L → AR-2 plan 3L → AR-3 code 3L, all CONVERGED): new `src/match-engine/tests/MatchEngineGkHeadingScenarios.cs` + `MatchEngineGkHeadingScenarioTests.cs` register `gk-heading-flag-on-composition` (Tier B; owning specs `{2,10,11,16,19}`; path under `SCENARIO_PATH_CROSS_SPEC_PREFIX`) — the Simulation-layer composition the unit suite does not exercise. Predicates: flag-on free-play forward determinism (two same-seed runs, byte-identical digest chains) + **flag-on restore-through-the-harness** (save@180 → `MatchSaveManager.Encode`/`Restore` in-memory blob → tick to 300 == uninterrupted flag-on run, which transitively locks Phase-2 v18 flag-on snapshot completeness) + structural on-pitch/finite invariants across a 5 s composed run (Agent Movement #2 bound) + the two projections firing as a **live consumer through the natural `RunTick` AI phase** (the scenario scripts only the world stimulus via `TestOnly_ForceBallLoose`, then lets `RunAiPhase → DriveGkHeadingTactical` commit — NOT the `TestOnly_Drive` seam) + a same-stimulus flag-off no-commit contrast (non-vacuous). No production change; no `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` change. **Full dotnet gate: PASSED, 0 failures (whole tree green; 305 match-engine tests; SDK 8.0.129 via apt).** **The DT-driven SAVE producer LANDED July 23, 2026 (ERR-008-013)** — the goalkeeper save is now a first-class Decision Tree (#8) action (`ActionType.SAVE = 7`, the DT-emitted save the #11 `SaveIntent` doc always anticipated), superseding the engine-side heuristic save trigger. Governed by the new converged supplement `docs/tracking/gk-heading-dt-producer-design.md` (Option-B outline AR-1 killed as unsound — the DT off-ball PRESS/INTERCEPT model run-to-the-ball, inverting the save; owner chose Option A; outline AR-2/AR-3, plan AR-4 (a real missed-save defect: `ScoreSave=ceiling` isn't robust — `INTERCEPT` can reach the clamp ceiling via `MentalityRiskMult`×`RoleWeightModifiers` and win the lower-ordinal tiebreak → fixed by emitting SAVE as the **sole** off-ball option)/AR-5, impl AR-6 all CONVERGED). New `TacticalContext.SaveAvailable` (set only for the threatened keeper under `EnableGkHeading`, from `GkHeadingIntentSource.SaveArmed`); `OptionGenerator` short-circuits the off-ball branch to SAVE alone; `UtilityScorer.ScoreSave` + a SAVE exemption from `PlayerTacticActionMultiplier` (its 7-wide #21 tables would OOB at ordinal 7); new `IDtSaveDispatch` seam + `ActionDispatcher` SAVE case + `DecisionTree` ctor param; `MatchEngine.HostSaveDispatch` maps agent→GK slot, applies the v18 latch, projects `ToGoalkeeper`, commits the same Stage-0 `SaveIntent`; `DriveGkHeadingTactical` drops `TryCommitSaveIntents` (header stays heuristic). SAVE is continuous for free (no state-machine edit); **no `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` change** (SAVE reuses `AgentAction.Type`/`TargetPosition`); flag-off byte-identical. New `OptionGeneratorTests` sole-option lock + `UtilityScorerTests` crash-guard lock + `MatchEngineGkHeadingTests` DT-path save-commit + `SaveDecision_SurvivesAdversarialTactic`. Spec back-prop `ERR-008-013` (spec-error-log.md v1.34) + `decision-tree/section-3-1.md`/`section-3-2.md` anchor notes. **Full dotnet gate: PASSED, 0 failures (whole tree green; DecisionTree 84, MatchEngine 306; SDK 8.0.129 via apt).** **`EnableGkHeading` default flipped to ON July 27, 2026** (§5.Z.15, as part of the match-realism pass below — `DisableGkHeading()` added for hosts/tests that want the old path), and the keeper given locomotion via the existing per-agent `AgentMovementSystem.Update` seam (no new locomotion model needed); flipping the default surfaced a real wiring gap (the engine booted the Pass/Shot/DecisionTree `EventBusRegistrar`s but never the Heading/Goalkeeper ones — fixed same commit). **Still deferred:** the `CollisionConsumer` AGENT_BALL duel fan-out (for contested headers), a DT-emitted HEADER (ordinal 8 overflows the 3-bit composure-noise field → rebaseline), and attribute-modulated / hesitant save commit — the save quality itself (not just its existence) is now the recorded next lever on the goal rate, see the match-realism OPEN ISSUES entry below.

Default path digest-locked byte-identical (KD-P7); distinct squads diverge by design, deterministically. **T3 COMPLETE (July 20, 2026):** the snapshot roster-reference field landed July 18 (v16, the identity half), and the **restore re-projection consumer** landed July 20 as snapshot-deserialize Phase 2 (`MatchEngine.cs` v1.42) — a match booted through `ConfigureSquads` with non-neutral records IS now restore-deterministic: `RestoreFromSnapshot` takes an `ISquadProvider`, and `ReprojectDistinctSquads` re-runs `LineupSelector` + `PlayerAttributeProjection` from the resolved roster and replays the substitution bench-swap keyed by the serialized `_activeBenchSlot` (KD-P10 / KD-T3-3 closed). - **Unity engine version bump (2022.3.62f1 → 6000.4.9f1, DX11) — RECERTIFIED July 19, 2026** — *opened July 13, 2026; recert complete July 19, 2026.* Project documentation updated to target Unity 6000.4.9f1 with graphics API DX11: `ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt`, `docs/tracking/certification-platform.md` (→ v1.3: Unity-version row updated, new Graphics-API row added, Status reverted `✅ PINNED` → `⏳ RECERT REQUIRED` per that file's own Maintenance Rule — a major Unity version bump invalidates the June 7, 2026 certification), `docs/tracking/cert-run-runbook.md` (→ v1.1: Step 0 pre-flight table updated), and this file's coding-conventions line ("Unity 2022 LTS conventions" → "Unity 6 LTS conventions"). **What this update deliberately did NOT do:** (1) run an actual certification against the new tuple — no host access was available, so every downstream unblocker `certification-platform.md` v1.1/v1.2 previously closed (`FR-DS-009-GATE` Stage 0 activation, `FR-PO-052` perf-gate, §7.5 D1 test-runner pin, `EnvironmentFingerprint` 6-field digest) is blocked again until one runs; (2) obtain Platform Certification owner sign-off, required by Spec #16 §1.7 before the pin can be marked ✅ again; (3) update `src/performance-optimization/CertifiedPerfBaseline.cs`'s `Stage0CertPlatformPin` constant (still hardcodes `win11-unity2022.3.62f1-mono-x64-sse4.2-1w-detflags`) or any other production code — that is a code change, not a documentation change, and is a prerequisite before `cert-run-runbook.md` Step 2 can execute against the new pin; (4) verify whether Unity 6000.4.9f1 actually changes the assumptions `tools/dotnet-ci`'s Linux compile/test shim bakes in about Unity's C# surface (`generate_projects.py` pins production `netstandard2.1` / `LangVersion 9.0` citing "Unity 2022.3's BCL surface" / "Unity 2022.3 C# level"; `tools/dotnet-ci/README.md` and `UnityShim.csproj` repeat the same claim) — if Unity 6 changed the default scripting API level or BCL target, the shim's fidelity claims would need re-verification against a real Unity 6 install, which is an engineering task outside this pass's scope; (5) touch dated, already-`APPROVED` spec section files that cite "Unity 2022.3 LTS" as reference hardware or as a formal citation (`positioning-ai/section-6.md`, `defensive-ai/section-6.md`, `attacking-ai/section-6.md`, `pressing-ai/section-6.md`, `deterministic-sim/section-5.md`, and the `[UNITY-LTS]`/`[UNITY-2022]` §8 citation blocks in `agent-movement`, `ball-physics`, `first-touch`, `collision-system`, `pass-mechanics`) — these are frozen approval-time records per this project's "historical rows preserved verbatim" convention, and retroactively editing them would misrepresent what was actually reviewed and signed off at approval time; a future spec-maintenance pass (with its own sign-off) would need to decide whether/how to re-cite Unity 6 in those specs. **Next steps, not done here:** schedule a real certification run against the new tuple per `cert-run-runbook.md` once host access exists; update the `CertifiedPerfBaseline` platform-pin string; decide whether the Mono backend / SSE4.2 / single-worker / deterministic-flags assumptions still hold under Unity 6 before re-marking the pin ✅. **Update (July 13, 2026, later same day — code follow-up landed):** the `CertifiedPerfBaseline.Stage0CertPlatformPin` code constant (step (3) above) is now DONE — it and its guard test (`CertifiedPerfBaselineTests.PlatformPinTokens_MatchDocumentedTuple`) encode `win11-unity6000.4.9f1-dx11-mono-x64-sse4.2-1w-detflags`; the constant's doc, `cert-run-runbook.md` (→ v1.2, Step 0 note + Step 2 citation), and `kickoff-multi-second.cert.md` (platform-pin row) updated to match. The token is updated so a first cert run stamps the correct pin — it does NOT assert a certified baseline exists. **Still open (cannot be done without host/human access):** (1) the actual certification run (needs pinned-host access to Windows 11 + Unity 6000.4.9f1 — no host in this environment); (2) Platform Certification owner sign-off (Spec #16 §1.7 — a human authority, not grantable by an agent); (4) authoritative re-verification of the `tools/dotnet-ci` shim's `netstandard2.1` / `LangVersion 9.0` claims against a real Unity 6000.4.9f1 install — investigated from documented Unity behaviour (Unity 6 with the Mono backend retains the .NET Standard 2.1 API Compatibility Level and C# 9 as its default scripting profile, so the shim's assumptions almost certainly still hold and the shim's technical claims are left UNCHANGED per this entry's item (4)), but confirming against an actual install remains an engineering task requiring the install, so this item is NOT closed and the shim was not edited. **Update (July 13, 2026, later same day — P1 real perf harness landed):** the `cert-run-runbook.md` **P1** prerequisite ("a real per-tick number requires the capstone scenario executing under a real harness … the Unity batch-mode command is a documented TBD") is now **Tier-A DONE**. New: `src/performance-optimization/StopwatchPerfHarness.cs` — the concrete `IPerfHarness` (§3.3.5 manual Stopwatch capture; nearest-rank p50/p99, which the spec left unpinned; single-threshold/loop-per-session capture; empty-corpus + double-session fail-closed guards; PassFail=Pass at capture per the `CertifiedPerfBaseline` precedent) with a new `src/performance-optimization/tests/` assembly (`performance-optimization-tests.asmdef` + `StopwatchPerfHarnessTests.cs`); and `src/match-engine/tests/MatchEngineCapstonePerfHarness.cs` — boots the real `MatchEngine` capstone and Stopwatch-times each of its `RunTick` calls (superseding the synthetic `tools/perf-harness/run.sh` `p50=0.000` stub that ran no `src/` code) + `MatchEngineCapstonePerfHarnessTests.cs` (asserts real execution ⇒ finite p50/p99 with p99 > 0; `TD_PERF_RUN_COUNT` env switches CI-fast 2 vs host 100). `MatchEngineCapstoneScenarios.NumTicks` promoted to public `KickoffMultiSecondTicks` (single source of truth). The Unity batch-mode command is now WRITTEN DOWN (`src/CLAUDE.md` "BUILD AND TEST COMMANDS"; `cert-run-runbook.md` Step 2 v1.3). **Deliberately NOT done (would be fabrication in this Linux/no-Unity environment):** the harness's Linux run is stamped `CertifiedPerfBaseline.LinuxNonCertPlatformPin` and produces NO certified number — the certified capture still needs P2 (pinned-host access) + runbook Steps 2–4 (capture 100 runs, promote PENDING→CERTIFIED, owner sign-off); and no `.meta` GUIDs / binary `ProjectSettings/*.asset` / `Library` were hand-produced (those come from opening the project in Unity on the host). So the two "Still open" items above (the actual cert run; owner sign-off) are unchanged — P1's *engineering* half is done, its *host-execution* half folds into P2. **UPDATE July 19, 2026 — RECERTIFICATION COMPLETE:** the pinned host became available and both cert runs executed on the Unity 6000.4.9f1 / DX11 / Mono tuple: (1) the **platform-determinism KAT run** — all golden-vector corpora + §5 tier locks byte-exact, 44/0 (`docs/specs/deterministic-sim/cert-runs/determinism-cert-2026-07-19.md`); (2) the **FR-PO-052 perf baseline** — 100-run capture, PENDING → CERTIFIED (`kickoff-multi-second.cert.md` v1.2). `certification-platform.md` → v1.4 **✅ PINNED** (all tuple rows), every downstream unblocker closed; owner sign-off recorded via the PR merges. - **Tactical-theory research cross-reference — four cheap-item additions LANDED** — *opened and closed July 7, 2026.* A conversation cross-referencing published soccer tactical theory (rest defence, marking orientation, half-spaces, cover-shadow/blind-side pressing, etc.) against the game plan identified several gaps whose extendibility cost was assessed as cheap-to-medium given the existing `TacticTranslation`/routing-field seam pattern (#21 Tactical Instructions T2). All four landed the same day, each defaulting to today's exact pre-addition behaviour (byte-identical) until a manager sets a non-default tactic: **(1) `MarkingOrientation` dial** (new `src/tactical-instructions/MarkingOrientation.cs`; `TeamTactic` gains the field, appended after `TimeWasting`; `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` 10 → 11) — BallOriented/Balanced/ManOriented scales the #14 MAN_MARK candidate radius via new `defensive-ai/TacticTranslation.MarkRadiusScalar` + `DefensiveSnapshot.MarkingOrientation` (ctor-seeded Balanced, since the enum's zero-value is BallOriented) + `MarkAssigner` consumption (FR-TI-033). **(2) Positioning AI #12 rest-defense coverage check** (new `src/positioning-ai/RestDefenseEvaluator.cs` + `Tests/RestDefenseEvaluatorTests.cs`, new §3.5/§7.13; `PositioningAITick.GetRestDefenseSufficient()`) — counts active outfield agents (GK excluded) behind `REST_DEFENSE_DEPTH_M` while `IN_POSSESSION`; insufficient coverage dampens PASS/SHOOT/DRIBBLE via new `TacticalContext.RestDefenseSufficient` (Stage0Default seeds `true`, since the zero-value `bool` default is `false`) + `TacticalWeights.RestDefenseRiskMult` in `UtilityScorer` (new #8 §3.2/§7.7). **(3) Half-spaces PASS bonus** (new #8 §3.2/§7.8) — `TacticalContext.AgentLane` routes each agent's EXISTING Positioning AI `LaneId` (already team-relative — no new axis-mirroring risk, unlike the historical `BallZone` home/away bugs) into the Decision Tree; `decision-tree.asmdef` gains the `TacticalDirector.PositioningAI` reference (first AI→Mechanics reference beyond `TacticalInstructions`); `TacticalWeights.LaneMult[5]` gives half-space lanes (LH/RH) a PASS bonus, central/wide stay ×1.0. **(4) Curving-press blind-side bias** (new `src/pressing-ai/BlindSideApproach.cs` + `Tests/BlindSideApproachTests.cs`, new #13 §7.12) — nudges the primary presser's approach target (post-selection only; who presses is unaffected) toward the ball carrier's blind side (opposite `PressingAgentSnapshot.Facing`, already carried for both teams) by `PressingAIConstants.BlindSideApproachBiasM`; wired into `PressingAITick` Step 3. New `MatchEngine.TestOnly_MarkingOrientation`/`_RestDefenseSufficient`/`_AgentLane` seams + `MatchEngineTacticTests` cases + `MatchEngineSnapshotSchemaTests.MarkingOrientation_FeedsSnapshotDigest` (schema pin 10→11). Spec docs updated: `tactical-instructions/section-2.md` (FR-TI-033 + field table) + `appendices.md` (Appendix B field order + A.3 constant row), `defensive-ai/section-3.md` (§3.3.3 Step 2 pseudocode), `positioning-ai/section-7.md` (new §7.13), `decision-tree/section-7.md` (new §7.7/§7.8), `pressing-ai/section-7.md` (new §7.12). Tracking docs updated: this file, `src/CLAUDE.md` v2.9, `file-manifest.md`, `README.md`. **Items considered but NOT implemented** (assessed as medium-large / genuinely new capability, not cheap seam-reuse): dismarking, scripted build-up-phase structures, positional rotations (dynamic slot/role swaps), and a full "game model" preset/AI-manager-tactic-selection layer — each would need its own spec pass rather than reusing an existing routing seam. **Adversarial review: AR-1 (0H+0M+1L, resolved same day)** — L: three new spec-section citations collided with pre-existing sections in the same specs' own `section-7.md` files (`§7.9` already "ML-Tuned [GT] Parameter Fitting" in positioning-ai; `§7.10`/`§7.11` did not exist yet in decision-tree but were about to via a different numbering pass) — renumbered to free slots (positioning-ai `§7.13`, decision-tree `§7.7`/`§7.8`) across every code comment, test, and spec-doc citation; pressing-ai's `§7.12` had no collision (its `§7.11` is Version History, `§7.12` was free) and was left as-is. **AR-2 (0H+0M+0L) — CONVERGENCE**: full re-check of default values (zero-value-enum traps on `MarkingOrientation`/`LaneId`/`bool RestDefenseSufficient`), snapshot-schema-version bump completeness, asmdef reference correctness (no cycles: `decision-tree → positioning-ai` is a valid AI→Mechanics reference per the layer taxonomy), per-agent vs per-team routing-order (Positioning AI's `Tick()` always runs before the per-agent `TacticalContext` build loop each stride, so `GetRestDefenseSufficient()`/`GetLane(i)` read the current tick's value, not stale), and test-assertion arithmetic (`REST_DEFENSE_DEPTH_M` = 105×0.40 = 42 m) found nothing further. **The four "considered but NOT implemented" items below are now scoped in two design supplements** — see the new OPEN ISSUES entry immediately below.

- **Advanced positional behaviors + game-model/AI-manager tactics — design supplements OPENED (candidate specs #23–#26)** — *opened July 7, 2026, later same day.* Follow-up to the entry above: the four items assessed there as "medium-large / genuinely new capability, not cheap seam-reuse" (dismarking, scripted build-up-phase structures, positional rotations, and the game-model/AI-manager preset-selection layer) are now scoped in two new design-supplement documents, at the same pre-promotion DESIGN SUPPLEMENT stage `tactical-instruction-layer-design.md` and `living-world-system-design.md` occupied before becoming Specs #21/#22 — **no code, no section files, not yet reviewed or approved.** **(1) `docs/tracking/advanced-positional-behaviors-design.md` v0.1 (→ v0.3 after AR-1 + implementation plan below)** — joint scoping note for dismarking (candidate **#23**), scripted build-up structures (candidate **#24**), and positional rotations (candidate **#25**); all three extend #7 Perception System / #8 Decision Tree / #12 Positioning AI. Candidate KD-2: dismarking must derive its marking-pressure signal from `PerceptionSystem.FilteredView` (proximity + dwell), never from reading the opposing team's internal `MarkAssignment.TargetEntityId` directly — an explicit **perception-boundary invariant** (candidate KD-5) that applies to all three items and should be cited verbatim in each eventual spec's §1. Candidate KD-4 flags positional rotations (dynamic `FormationSlotRecord`/`SlotIndex` reassignment mid-match — nothing today reassigns a slot after `SeedFromFormation`) as the largest and riskiest of the three. **(2) `docs/tracking/game-model-ai-manager-design.md` v0.1 (→ v0.4 after AR-1/AR-2 + implementation plan below)** — scoping note for a tactical-preset library over the existing `TeamTacticConfig`/`PlayerTacticConfig` substrate (Spec #21; already parser-swap-ready and default-identity-safe) plus AI-manager selection/adaptation logic, staged as candidate **#26** (one spec, T0–T4 phases, mirroring #21's own T-phase staging — not two specs, since both halves share one consumer path through the existing `SetTeamTactic`/`SetPlayerTactic` appliers). Candidate KD-2: manager decisions run on a new, coarser cadence (kickoff / half-time / fixed interval) — never every 10 Hz AI stride. Candidate KD-4 (mirrors positional-behaviors KD-5): a manager AI may not read an opponent's private `PlayerTactic` state directly; opponent-aware adaptation is explicitly flagged as a candidate deferral to avoid building a consumer for canon that doesn't exist yet (the same FR-LW-031 phantom-interface reasoning `BackgroundTierSim` cites). **Candidate spec numbers #23–#26 are reserved in both design notes' own §5 tables to prevent a future renumbering collision, but are deliberately NOT added to `SPEC_INDEX.md`'s registry table yet** — per that file's own "Before creating a new spec folder, add the entry here first" rule and the #21/#22 precedent (registry row added only at promotion to section files, not at design-note stage); `SPEC_INDEX.md` instead gains a small NOTES-section reservation line so the numbers cannot be silently reused. **Adversarial review of the design-supplement text: AR-1 (0H+0M+2L, resolved same day; findings

- **Tactical-theory research cross-reference — four cheap-item additions LANDED** — *opened and closed July 7, 2026.* A conversation cross-referencing published soccer tactical theory (rest defence, marking orientation, half-spaces, cover-shadow/blind-side pressing, etc.) against the game plan identified several gaps whose extendibility cost was assessed as cheap-to-medium given the existing `TacticTranslation`/routing-field seam pattern (#21 Tactical Instructions T2). All four landed the same day, each defaulting to today's exact pre-addition behaviour (byte-identical) until a manager sets a non-default tactic: **(1) `MarkingOrientation` dial** (new `src/tactical-instructions/MarkingOrientation.cs`; `TeamTactic` gains the field, appended after `TimeWasting`; `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` 10 → 11) — BallOriented/Balanced/ManOriented scales the #14 MAN_MARK candidate radius via new `defensive-ai/TacticTranslation.MarkRadiusScalar` + `DefensiveSnapshot.MarkingOrientation` (ctor-seeded Balanced, since the enum's zero-value is BallOriented) + `MarkAssigner` consumption (FR-TI-033). **(2) Positioning AI #12 rest-defense coverage check** (new `src/positioning-ai/RestDefenseEvaluator.cs` + `Tests/RestDefenseEvaluatorTests.cs`, new §3.5/§7.13; `PositioningAITick.GetRestDefenseSufficient()`) — counts active outfield agents (GK excluded) behind `REST_DEFENSE_DEPTH_M` while `IN_POSSESSION`; insufficient coverage dampens PASS/SHOOT/DRIBBLE via new `TacticalContext.RestDefenseSufficient` (Stage0Default seeds `true`, since the zero-value `bool` default is `false`) + `TacticalWeights.RestDefenseRiskMult` in `UtilityScorer` (new #8 §3.2/§7.7). **(3) Half-spaces PASS bonus** (new #8 §3.2/§7.8) — `TacticalContext.AgentLane` routes each agent's EXISTING Positioning AI `LaneId` (already team-relative — no new axis-mirroring risk, unlike the historical `BallZone` home/away bugs) into the Decision Tree; `decision-tree.asmdef` gains the `TacticalDirector.PositioningAI` reference (first AI→Mechanics reference beyond `TacticalInstructions`); `TacticalWeights.LaneMult[5]` gives half-space lanes (LH/RH) a PASS bonus, central/wide stay ×1.0. **(4) Curving-press blind-side bias** (new `src/pressing-ai/BlindSideApproach.cs` + `Tests/BlindSideApproachTests.cs`, new #13 §7.12) — nudges the primary presser's approach target (post-selection only; who presses is unaffected) toward the ball carrier's blind side (opposite `PressingAgentSnapshot.Facing`, already carried for both teams) by `PressingAIConstants.BlindSideApproachBiasM`; wired into `PressingAITick` Step 3.

  **CORRECTED/REVERTED after user review, same day** — the user identified items (2)/(3)/(4) as architecturally wrong designs, not bugs. **(2) redesigned**: the dampener must not be an omniscient flat team-wide penalty; it now scales by the ball carrier's own tactical awareness (mean of `A_Decisions`/`A_Anticipation`) via `Mathf.Lerp(1.0f, RestDefenseRiskMult, awareness)` in `UtilityScorer` — an unaware carrier takes the risky action anyway (a genuine tactical/setup flaw for the manager to fix, not the AI to silently correct). **(3) REVERTED entirely**: half-spaces are an exploitable spatial gap requiring tactical/player instructions to exploit, not a flat passing bonus; `TacticalContext.AgentLane`, `TacticalWeights.LaneMult`, and the `decision-tree.asmdef` to `PositioningAI` reference are all removed (decision-tree/section-7.md §7.8 marked REVERTED, not deleted). **(4) redesigned**: curving press runs are for bending the pursuit path to adjust COVER SHADOW (deny a nearby passing option while closing down), not for approaching the carrier's blind side; `BlindSideApproach.cs` DELETED, replaced by `src/pressing-ai/CoverShadowCurve.cs` (`PressingAIConstants.CoverCurveBlendWeightMax`) whose effectiveness is gated by the presser's own attributes (new `PressingAgentSnapshot.DefensivePositioningAttribute`/`PhysicalEffortAttribute`/`MentalSharpnessAttribute`, sourced by `MatchEngine` from the same `_dtAttrs` the Decision Tree reads) — a poor, low-effort defender barely curves at all.

  New `MatchEngine.TestOnly_MarkingOrientation`/`_RestDefenseSufficient` seams + `MatchEngineTacticTests` cases + `MatchEngineSnapshotSchemaTests.MarkingOrientation_FeedsSnapshotDigest` (schema pin 10→11). It constructs/wires the six existing services (`WorldClock`+`MemoryStore`+`ColdStore`+`ArcEngine`+`ActiveSetMembership`+`WorldLoop`) for one manager, drives one calendar day per `AdvanceDay` (the §4.2 phases with producers run — 3 decay, 4 arc expiry, 6 external-cap LRU; phases 1 match-outcome ingest / 2 vol-2 read / 5 background-tier stay the WorldLoop's documented null seams, FR-LW-031 — no phantom invented), and `RecordInteraction` stamps the episode with the store's current day. `Snapshot()`/`Restore()` round-trip the whole store as one canonical payload: a fail-loud composite header (`WORLD_STORE_FORMAT_VERSION` + `DomainTagLivingWorld` + managerId) + the existing §4.6 four-store block (`WorldStateSerializer`, bitwise via #16 CanonicalSerializer) + the FR-LW-022 active-set membership roster (entityId + isOwnClub per member — the one piece the §4.6 block does not carry; its snapshot entry was flagged "a follow-up", this is it), with fail-loud gates on version/tag/managerId/length-count-prefix (a `ReadCount` parallel to the serializer's)/membership-flag/trailing bytes; Restore rebuilds through the validating store seams and re-wires the loop. `ActiveSetMembership` v1.3 gains the roster seam (`MemberCount`/`GetMemberAt`/`RestoreMember`, the last validated against the rebuilt `MemoryStore` for the membership↔edge live-edge invariant); `LivingWorldConstants` gains `[FIXED] WORLD_STORE_FORMAT_VERSION`. New `Tests/WorldStoreTests.cs` (14 tests): wiring, AdvanceDay decay+arc-expiry across the lifetime boundary, current-tick stamping, idempotent+field-identical + empty-store round-trip, two-run determinism, six fail-loud restore gates. Note the generator's cursor lives in the WorldStore's own composite save, NOT yet in the unified match/season save (that fold stays blocked on FR-LW-003 + the season save-file root). **Slice-5 AR-1 (July 3, 2026): 0H+0M+3L, all resolved** — L-1: `Restore` now fails loud on a non-zero `RestoreStream` code (was silently resuming the text stream at cursor 0 under a future registration-order change); L-2: documented the atomic-`Generate` invariant that lets the block omit the reservation fields; L-3: header byte-order corrected.
