See src/CLAUDE.md v2.1–v2.2. **Slice 6 — deep-memory→text auto-cite (July 3, 2026):** a new `WorldStore.GenerateInteractionText(intent, entityId, subject, opponent, homeGoals, awayGoals)` overload pulls the contact's most-salient **citable** §3.2 episode from the store's own `MemoryStore` (the manager→entityId edge) and cites it — the §3.3 "cite a §3.2 episode" path (FR-LW-013) that until now had no composition-root consumer (the slots overload left the caller to hand-build the cited episode). Selection scans the edge's live `Memory` buffer for the highest-salience episode with a citable kind (not `EventKind.None`) clearing `SALIENCE_REF_THRESHOLD` (negated-compare fail-closed on the NaN the store seams already reject); deterministic tiebreak salience→worldTick→episodeId; no qualifier ⇒ no citation. Exactly one `world.text` draw either way, so the selection is a pure function of the serialized memory and resumes byte-identically across `Snapshot`/`Restore` — **no new serialized state, no `WORLD_STORE_FORMAT_VERSION` bump** (the episode is read from the already-serialized MemoryStore). `Tests/WorldStoreTests.cs` +4 (fresh-episode cite; full-tie episodeId tiebreak; no-edge omits citation; save-boundary auto-cite determinism). See src/CLAUDE.md v2.3. **Slice 7 — arc-trigger evaluator + `world.arcs` sub-stream LANDED (July 24, 2026; Slice 1/E1 + Slice 2/E2):** the KD-10 "arc trigger evaluators + first draw site" the slice-2 note deferred, per the converged supplement `docs/tracking/living-world-arc-triggers-design.md` (v0.5, Path B — opt-in, default-off, byte-neutral). Rather than the phantom-interface surface FR-LW-031 forbade at slice 2 (a `world.arcs` stream with zero draw sites reading vol-2/vol-3 canon that does not exist), the trigger signals now arrive through a concrete, nullable, caller-supplied `ArcCanonSource` (KD-1/KD-2 — a single concrete class + nested `Builder`, NOT an `IArcCanonSource` interface): a flag-off `WorldStore` never constructs one and is byte-identical to pre-slice-7, while a caller that attaches one opts the world into arc spawning. New `src/living-world/` files: `ArcCanonSource.cs`, `ArcTrigger.cs` / `ArcTriggerCatalogue.cs` (the APPEND-only Stage-0 table — two entity-scoped rows (WonderkidVsVeteran ego-clash thr 0.75/45d; MediaVendetta media-hostility thr 0.70/60d) + two board rows in ArcKind ordinal order (DressingRoomSplit pulse-divergence thr 0.65/30d; BoardPatienceCollapse board-impatience thr 0.80/90d)), `ArcTriggerEvaluator.cs` (registers `world.arcs` under a **distinct** entity sentinel `WORLD_STREAM_ENTITY_ARCS = -2` so `ComputeStreamKey` differs from `world.text`'s -1 — KD-4, the collision hazard; walks the catalogue in FR-LW-017/021 order applying the **KD-7 rising-edge armed-off latch** — a sustained signal fires exactly once and drop-below re-arms; one `world.arcs` draw per rising edge; resolves a minimal FR-LW-018 pin (entity: most-salient episode on the manager→entity edge; board/edge-less: none) then `ArcEngine.SpawnArc`). `WorldLoop` gains a 5th nullable seam + `RunWorldTick(ArcCanonSource canon = null)` (KD-5, per-tick argument, no stale-canon field); `WorldStore` owns the evaluator + a nullable `_canon` (`SetArcCanon` / a canon ctor overload / `Restore(payload, canon)` thread it, never persisted — the `ISquadProvider` Load-time-parameter precedent); `season-save/SeasonSaveManager.Load` gains an optional `ArcCanonSource canon` threaded into `WorldStore.Restore` (§8.9(a)) so a flag-on world keeps evaluating through the season file. **Slice 1/E1:** a flag-on run is deterministic FORWARD but `Snapshot()` fails loud (not yet snapshot-safe); no `WORLD_STORE_FORMAT_VERSION` change (default path byte-identical). **Slice 2/E2:** `Snapshot`/`Restore` serialize the `world.arcs` cursor + the KD-7 latch (a canonical-order block — `EnumerateLatchedCanonical` sorts by scopeKey then triggerId — with a strict-order/no-dup `RestoreLatched` fail-loud gate) between the `world.text` block and the membership roster, **`WORLD_STORE_FORMAT_VERSION` 2 → 3** (v2 payloads rejected fail-loud, no migration); the E1 fail-loud is dropped, so a flag-on `save@N → Restore(canon) → advance to N+K` is byte-identical to the uninterrupted run AND a still-latched trigger does NOT re-fire on restore (the re-fire-after-restore bug the latch serialization exists to prevent). New `Tests/ArcTriggerTests.cs` (design §9 tests 1–4/4b/4c/6–11 + a latch enumerate/restore seam test) + `SeasonSaveManagerTests` +1 (flag-on world resumes evaluating through the season file, with a null-canon negative control asserting divergence). **Full dotnet gate: PASSED, 0 failures (SDK 8.0.129 via apt; whole tree green — 118 living-world tests, 20 season-save). Slice-1 default path byte-identical (no version change); Slice-2 comparative round-trip, no absolute rebaseline.** See src/CLAUDE.md v2.34. **Still open:** **BackgroundTierSim summary updates (§3.5) — remains the documented WorldLoop phase-5 seam, deliberately NOT built:** it summarises club-AI/transfer/sacking outcomes that do not exist yet (building a consumer is the phantom-interface class FR-LW-031 forbids — the same reasoning that keeps `world.arcs` unregistered in this same task), and §3.5 specifies no background-tier update formula to implement against; the composite `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump that folds the §4.6 block into the *match/season* snapshot (still deferred — the WorldStore now owns the living-world composite save, but folding it into the unified match/season save is blocked because FR-LW-003 bars the match engine from referencing this assembly and the unified season save-file root does not exist yet — note the `world.text` cursor now lives in the WorldStore's *own* composite save per Slice 5, so only the fold into the *unified* match/season save remains); and the KD-10 upstreams owned elsewhere (vol-2/vol-3 human-systems; structured match-outcome events).

- **UI / Client Framework (#38) — T0 substrate LANDED; Wave-7 screens + the UGUI binding remain open** — *opened and landed July 25, 2026.* The approved-but-unbuilt §3.1–§3.3 contracts now exist in a new host-free presentation assembly `src/ui-framework/` (`TacticalDirector.UiFramework`), governed by `docs/tracking/ui-framework-t0-implementation-plan.md` (v0.3; plan AR-1 3M+3L → code AR-1 1H+3M+4L → code AR-2 1M+2L, all resolved). Landed: the `IViewModelSource<T>` projection contract, the `NavigationShell` deterministic stack machine, `ICommandDispatcher` + `ManagerIntent` + the one concrete `MatchTacticsDispatcher` (routing ONLY to the three verified public seams `SetTeamTactic` / `SetPlayerTactic` / `SubstitutePlayer`), and the one concrete match projection (`MatchFrameView` + `MatchViewModelSource` over a new `ILiveFrameSource` seam). 39 tests incl. the two locks that keep the layer honest — observer neutrality (a 240-tick digest chain is byte-identical whether or not the UI projects) and a mechanical reverse-reference scan over every `src/**/*.asmdef` (FR-UI-001). **Full dotnet gate PASSED, whole tree green.** No `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` change; no RNG stream / domain tag / ordinal (FR-UI-022). **Three deliberate deviations from the approved spec text, each back-propped** (`spec-error-log.md`): **ERR-038-001** — live-match marshaling reuses the shipped `ManagerCommandQueue` + pre-tick drain rather than §3.3/§4.1's proposed `LiveMatchStreamer.EnqueueIntent`, which would have given the SHARED streamer a mutation surface and regressed the browser viewer's playback-only invariant (the interactive-Unity-client AR-1 H-2 finding, settled one day after #38's approval); FR-UI-023 is satisfied identically. **ERR-038-002** — §4.1's "generic substrate references nothing sim-side" cannot hold literally, since §2.2's own `ManagerIntent` carries `TeamTactic`/`PlayerTactic`/`SubstitutionReason`; the load-bearing invariants are FR-UI-001 + FR-UI-003, both preserved. **ERR-038-003** — `Register` refuses a duplicate `ScreenId` instead of §3.2's silent overwrite. **Still open:** the **UGUI rendering binding** (Unity-host-gated, §4.3/§7.2 — the same gate the match viewer hit) and every **Wave-7 screen** (§7.1, each gated on its data spec existing). Of the screens the July-25 mockups cover, only **Tactics (S1)** and **Squad** have built data behind them today (#21 + #27); transfers / training / scouting / finances / staff are gated on #31 / #29 / #32 / #40 / #34, none of which have any `src/` — a screen against them would be the phantom-consumer trap KD-2 forbids. Each screen also needs its own Wave-7 spec before code, per #38 §7.1.

- **Presentation layer — minimal match viewer LANDED; interactive Unity client remains open** — *opened July 2, 2026.* `src/match-viewer/` (`TacticalDirector.MatchViewer`; presentation tooling, not a numbered spec — the UI layer proper stays Stage 1+ per the src/CLAUDE.md layer taxonomy) records a real `MatchEngine` run and exports a **self-contained HTML canvas replay**: ball + 22 agents with team/GK/possession/ball-height cues, play/pause/scrub/speed controls, pitch markings from an IFAB `[FIXED]` catalogue (`MatchViewerConstants.cs`). Enabled by the `MatchEngine.cs` v1.24 public observation surface (read-only value-type copies; `MatchViewerTests` digest-locks observer neutrality — recording a run is byte-identical to not observing it — plus bitwise two-run determinism, on-pitch finiteness, cadence, and fail-loud guards incl. the NaN-gate on export). The HTML output is explicitly NOT a determinism-pinned wire format (rounded display coordinates only; same contract class as the Stage-0 tactic text grammar). **AR-1 (July 2, 2026, same day): 0H+2M+3L, all resolved** — M-1 `MatchReplay` incoherent-frame refusal (canvas silently no-opped short/long data rows); M-2 observation-surface roster-index guards (`MatchEngine.cs` v1.25); L: exposed-array hand-over doc, non-uniform final-interval playback timing, `0:60.0` clock rounding + contiguous-roster jersey assumption (see src/CLAUDE.md v1.86). **AR-2 (July 2, 2026, later same day): full-surface sweep, 0H+3M+3L, all resolved** — M: empty-replay refusal (exported player crashed at first render), non-monotonic-tick refusal (per-interval timing needs dt > 0), `MatchViewerConstants` [GT] rows migrated onto `GameplayConfig` (June-30 catalogue-migration conformity; recorder default-stride params → explicit overloads); L: `MatchReplay` roster-index guards (the AR-1 M-2 fix had not reached the sibling surface), ball-marker radius clamp (negative-z ⇒ `arc()` IndexSizeError killed all rendering), metadata NaN-gates (see src/CLAUDE.md v1.87). **AR-3 (July 2, 2026, latest same day): 0H+1M+2L, all resolved** — M: the frames `ReadOnlyCollection` wrapped the caller's LIVE list (a view), so post-construction `frames.Add(...)` bypassed every ctor guard; now snapshot-copied before validation. L: `PossessingAgentId` range-validated; empty roster refused (src/CLAUDE.md v1.88). Convergence: AR-3 found no High and nothing in the recorder/exporter/JS/engine-surface — findings confined to the public `MatchReplay` ctor validation set. **AR-4 (July 2, 2026): 0H+0M+3L, all resolved — CONVERGENCE (L-only round)** — Space-key toggle no longer hijacks focused controls; `Export` gates the config-resolved [GT] presentation values (post-AR-2-M-3 a malformed config could emit a silently broken document); doc-contract fixes (`Tick` pre-run engines, `SampleStride` provenance-only). AR cycle closed per the project convention (src/CLAUDE.md v1.89). **Still open:** the interactive Unity client (live rendering, input, match presentation) — the viewer is replay-only; the first cert run on the pinned Windows/Unity tuple (`certification-platform.md` v1.2) remains the recommended precursor before investing in it. **UI mockups landed July 25, 2026** (`docs/design/ui-mockups/` — a design-system page + 11 screen mockups + shared assets, imported as the visual reference for UI / Client Framework #38 and the Wave-7 screen specs it defers to). They are **non-normative**: static HTML/CSS/JS with hardcoded illustrative data, not on any build path, not read by the sim, not in any snapshot/digest — the spec wins on any disagreement. Two visual directions ship side by side in `tokens.css`; **`touchline` (analyst tool) was CHOSEN July 25, 2026** — `stadium` is retained as the rejected alternative behind the mockups' switcher, and `assets/app.js`'s default was corrected from `stadium` to `touchline` (every page declared `data-direction="touchline"` in its markup while the script overrode it on load, so the mockups had been rendering a direction they did not claim). The `touchline` token block is what the eventual client theme derives from; nothing in it is `[GT]`-tagged or read by the sim.

- **Match Engine integration (Phases A–F) — COMPLETE** — *closed June 28, 2026 (opened June 27, 2026).* `src/match-engine/` is Stage 0+1 integration scaffolding (design note `docs/tracking/match-engine-design.md`; not a numbered spec — does not introduce #23). Phases A–E (composition root + boot; full canonical world-state snapshot serialization at `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` 8 — ball/agent state, executor+context state, all four mechanics-AI hysteresis surfaces, per-agent `DecisionTreeState`, Perception internal state; Physics/Resolve phase wiring for Collision #3, Pass #5 / Shot #6 executors, First Touch #4; AI phase wiring Positioning #12 → Pressing #13 → Defensive #14 → Attacking #15 → DecisionTree #8 with the ERR-008-002 home/away mirror guard at every team-mapped seam; Events-phase possession-changed producer/consumer with `EventBus.ResetForNewMatch()`) plus **Phase F (capstone)** are implemented and tested. Phase F (June 28, 2026): `src/match-engine/tests/MatchEngineCapstoneScenarios.cs` registers `match-engine-kickoff-multi-second` on the #19 `ScenarioRunner` (owning specs `{1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,12,13,14,15,16,17,19}`, Tier B) — it boots a real `MatchEngine`, ticks it 600× (10 s @ 60 Hz) through all 7 phases, and records gameplay-invariant envelope predicates (tick count; `ai-stride-cadence` = `NumTicks / AI_PHASE_STRIDE` = 100, locking the 10 Hz/60 Hz loop separation; ball + agents finite and on-pitch every tick; chained snapshot digest advances every tick) plus a two-run same-seed determinism digest match (also re-locks `EventBus.ResetForNewMatch()` across two in-process matches). `MatchEngineCapstoneTests.cs` runs it through `ScenarioRunner.Run`, adds a direct two-run digest-chain equality test, and activates the **FR-PO-052** per-tick perf gate via `PerfGateRunner.Run` against a generous Stage-0 anchor baseline (NON-certifying Linux gate; the authoritative per-tick budget stays on the pinned Windows/Unity tuple per `certification-platform.md` v1.2). No production `MatchEngine.cs` change — the scenario reads world state through existing internal `TestOnly_*` seams + the public `CurrentTick`/`AiPhaseRunCount`/`CurrentSnapshotDigest`. **FR-PO-052 certified baseline corpus machinery (June 28, 2026):** the capstone's perf gate runs against a *generous in-code Stage-0 anchor*; the certified-baseline layer it stands in for now exists — `src/performance-optimization/CertifiedPerfBaseline.cs` (+ `CertificationStatus.cs`) models a corpus entry as Pending or Certified. The kickoff entry is **Pending** at Stage 0 (carries no metric, refuses to build a `BaselineRecord`) because a certified number must come from the pinned Windows/Unity tuple and the Linux gate is explicitly NON-certifying (no fabricated number); `Certified(...)` validates a complete `SessionManifest` + finite positive metrics and projects to a corpus `BaselineRecord` for `PerfGateRunner`/`RegressionGate`. First on-disk corpus artifact `docs/specs/performance-optimization/baselines/match-engine/kickoff-multi-second.cert.md` (PENDING_CERT_RUN + promotion runbook). The authoritative per-tick budget still requires a cert run on the pinned tuple (`certification-platform.md` v1.2). Tracked in `match-engine-design.md` §5 Phase F + Version History v1.0 / v1.3.

- **Tactical Instructions (#21) — APPROVED** — *June 20, 2026.* First Stage-1 forward spec beyond the Stage-0 set of 20. Manager-facing tactical instruction input layer (formation/mentality/team instructions/player roles+duties/individual instructions) that drives the existing AI subsystems (#8/#11–#15). Promoted from `docs/tracking/tactical-instruction-layer-design.md` v0.3 (three adversarial passes) to `docs/specs/tactical-instructions/` (11 section files); PASS-1 (2H+4M+4L) + PASS-2 (1H+4M+1L) section-file adversarial reviews resolved to v0.3; lead-developer R-01..R-05 sign-off granted. 32 FRs (FR-TI-001..032); XC-021-001..014; no RNG / no domain tag (KD-6). **Post-APPROVED follow-ups (non-blocking, Stage-1 implementation-time):** (i) **G2 balance pass** — the `RoleWeightModifiers` + §3.2 Mentality `[GT]` magnitudes in §3/Appendix A are illustrative; a numerical-mirror + balance review pins them at Stage-1 (the spec's contract is the shapes/directions, which were reviewed). Precedent: #8 draft-level approval, #9 §9.8, #16 post-approved items. (ii) ERR-021-001..004 cross-spec back-props (#8 `TacticalContext` carrier, #16 snapshot field block + `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION`, #14 man-mark seam, #19 `T-TI-*` prefixes) land at their named stage. **Runtime activation** is separately gated on the `[GT]` config-loader + match-engine Phase C/D (KD-8) — neither owned by #21. **T0 scaffolding landed June 21, 2026** (`src/tactical-instructions/`: 16 enums + `TeamTactic`/`PlayerInstructions`/`PlayerTactic` + identity factories + `TacticalInstructionsConstants` + ordinal/identity tests; behaviour-neutral per KD-10; asmdef `references` empty until `project-constants` exists). Remaining #21 implementation: T2–T3 seams (consumer-side `TacticTranslation` maps + routing fields on `TacticalContext` / #12–#15 snapshots), which land as each consumer is wired at match-engine Phase D. **T2 Decision Tree (#8) seam landed June 28, 2026** (behaviour-neutral): `src/decision-tree/TacticTranslation.cs` (TacticPressing/TacticPassing → #8 PressingMode/PassingStyle, rank-mapped so the opposite enum orderings do not invert; §3.1 F5 widening-clamp; §3.2 Mentality risk/line resolvers off `TacticalInstructionsConstants`), a `Mentality` routing field on `TacticalContext` (`Stage0Default` seeds Balanced = identity, FR-TI-031; the zero-value struct default is `VeryDefensive`, so consume only via the factory — same contract as `Pressing`/`DefensiveLineDepth`), and the §3.2/§3.3 per-option Mentality risk multiplier wired into `UtilityScorer.ComputeUtility` before the clamp (Balanced ⇒ ×1.0, unchanged until the Phase-D writer routes a live tactic). `decision-tree(.Tests).asmdef` gain the `TacticalInstructions` reference; `TacticTranslationTests.cs` added. **Runtime activation (Phase-D single-writer for #8) landed June 28, 2026** (behaviour-neutral): the match engine now holds a per-team `TeamTactic` (`_active`/`_pendingTeamTactics`, default `TeamTactic.Balanced` = identity), exposes a public `MatchEngine.SetTeamTactic(teamId, tactic)` that stages a pending change, commits pending→active at the AI-stride boundary (FR-TI-027), and `RunMechanicsAI` overlays the active tactic's `Mentality` (drives the §3.2/§3.3 `UtilityScorer` risk mult) + translated `Pressing`/`Passing` (via the now-`public` `TacticTranslation`) into each agent's `TacticalContext`. A default match is byte-identical to pre-#21 (Balanced ⇒ MEDIUM/MIXED/×1.0 = `Stage0Default`); a non-Balanced tactic lights up both the new Mentality multiplier and the pre-existing `TacticalModifierResolver` Pressing/Passing modifiers. `match-engine(.Tests).asmdef` gain the `TacticalInstructions` ref; `MatchEngineTacticTests.cs` added (routing per team + translation + FR-TI-027 stride-gating + Balanced behaviour-neutrality + determinism). The remaining activation gate is the `[GT]` config-loader (the disk source that would *populate* `SetTeamTactic`); at Stage 0 the tactic is set in-code before kickoff. **T2 Pressing AI (#13) consumer seam landed June 28, 2026** (behaviour-neutral): `src/pressing-ai/TacticTranslation.cs` resolves `LineOfEngagement` → a multiplicative scalar on the #13 press-trigger radius (`PressTriggerDistanceM`) via `LineOfEngagementScalar` — a DIRECT ordinal lookup (the #21 and #13 orderings already agree, unlike the #8 rank-remap) with the §3.1 F5 widening clamp; Standard ⇒ ×1.0 (FR-TI-031). A `LineOfEngagement` routing field on `PressingSnapshot` is constructor-seeded `Standard` (= identity — the zero-value enum default is `VeryLow` ×0.80, so the explicit seed is load-bearing for behaviour-neutrality), and `PrimaryPressSelector.Select` scales its eligibility radius by that scalar before the squared compare (×1.0 at Standard ⇒ `triggerDistSq` byte-identical to pre-#21; the field's sole production consumer). `pressing-ai(.Tests).asmdef` gain the `TacticalInstructions` ref; `pressing-ai/Tests/TacticTranslationTests.cs` added (scalar validity + Standard identity + ctor-seed behaviour-neutrality + monotone shape + F5 clamp). **The #13 match-engine Phase-D writer landed June 29, 2026** (behaviour-neutral): `MatchEngine.FillPressingSnapshot` now routes the pressing team's active `TeamTactic.LineOfEngagement` into `PressingSnapshot.LineOfEngagement` (overwriting the ctor `Standard` seed) — the #13 analogue of the #8 v1.16 single-writer. Default Balanced ⇒ Standard ⇒ ×1.0 trigger-radius scalar ⇒ byte-identical to pre-#21. New `TestOnly_PressLineOfEngagement(teamId)` seam; `MatchEngineTacticTests.cs` v1.1 adds per-team routing + Standard-default cases. MatchEngine.cs v1.18. **The #12/#14/#15 consumer seams landed June 29, 2026** (behaviour-neutral): each Mechanics consumer gains an `<assembly>/TacticTranslation.cs` + a snapshot routing field seeded to identity, repeating the #8/#13 pattern. **#12 Positioning AI (active-neutral, fully wired):** `positioning-ai/TacticTranslation.cs` maps `TacticWidth`/`TacticDefWidth` → lateral-compactness scalar (`WidthScalar`/`DefWidthScalar`, direct ordinal lookup + §3.1 F5 clamp; both `Standard` rows = ×1.00); `ContextModifierInputs` gains `Width`/`DefensiveWidth` fields (3-arg ctor seeds `Standard` so every existing caller stays byte-identical — zero-value enum defaults are VeryNarrow/Narrow; new 5-arg ctor for the Phase-D writer); `ContextModifier.ApplyToAll` multiplies `lateralScale` by the phase-selected width scalar (in-poss → Width, OOP → DefensiveWidth), ×1.00 exact ⇒ byte-identical to pre-#21. **#14 Defensive AI (routing seam, consumption deferred):** `defensive-ai/TacticTranslation.cs` (`OffsideTrapRequested` bool passthrough); `DefensiveSnapshot.OffsideTrapRequested` defaults to the `false` identity. Per KD-9 the toggle is a *request, never a guarantee* — gating today's autonomous §3.7.2 trap arming behind a default-false toggle would NOT be behaviour-neutral, so `OffsideTrapController` is unchanged and the arming-gate consumption lands with the Phase-D writer + activation. **#15 Attacking AI (routing seam, consumption deferred):** `attacking-ai/TacticTranslation.cs` maps `FocusPlay` → preferred `Flank?` (Mixed/ThroughMiddle → null identity); `AttackingSnapshot.FocusPlay` auto-property zero-value is `Mixed` = identity; `OverloadDetector` flank-preference consumption deferred. Each consuming assembly's production + tests asmdef gains the `TacticalInstructions` ref; new `Tests/TacticTranslationTests.cs` in each (catalogue mapping + identity row + monotone shape + F5 clamp + default-snapshot seed). **The #14/#15 match-engine Phase-D writers landed June 29, 2026** (behaviour-neutral; MatchEngine.cs v1.19): `FillDefensiveSnapshot` routes the active `TeamTactic.OffsideTrap` → `DefensiveSnapshot.OffsideTrapRequested` via fully-qualified `TacticalDirector.DefensiveAI.TacticTranslation` (the #13 v1.17 CS0104 lesson — five `TacticTranslation` types now in match-engine scope), and `FillAttackingSnapshot` routes the active `TeamTactic.FocusPlay` → `AttackingSnapshot.FocusPlay` (enum passthrough; the consumer translates to `Flank?`). Default Balanced ⇒ false / Mixed = the routing identities, byte-identical to pre-#21. **Active consumption stays deferred** exactly as the routing-field docs state: #14 `OffsideTrapController` per KD-9 (gating its autonomous §3.7.2 arming behind a default-false toggle would not be neutral — it lands with the §3.7.2 additive-request design at activation); #15 `OverloadDetector` flank-preference per the §5.6/G2 balance pass. New `TestOnly_OffsideTrapRequested(teamId)` / `TestOnly_FocusPlay(teamId)` seams; `MatchEngineTacticTests.cs` v1.2 adds per-team routing + identity-default cases. **The #12 match-engine Phase-D writer landed June 29, 2026** (behaviour-neutral; MatchEngine.cs v1.20) — the last of the three Mechanics writers: `RunMechanicsAI` now builds `ContextModifierInputs` via the 5-arg ctor, routing the active `TeamTactic.Width` / `DefensiveWidth` (which `ContextModifier` translates to the in-poss / OOP lateral-compactness scalar). Default Balanced ⇒ Standard / Standard ⇒ scalar 1.00 ⇒ byte-identical to pre-#21 (the 5-arg both-Standard ctor ≡ the 3-arg identity ctor). The modifier struct is a transient per-tick input, captured per-team in `_posModifiers` only for the new `TestOnly_PositioningWidth` / `TestOnly_PositioningDefWidth` seams; `MatchEngineTacticTests.cs` v1.3 adds per-team routing + Standard-default cases. **All three Mechanics Phase-D writers (#12/#13/#14/#15) are now closed.** **Active #14/#15 consumption landed June 29, 2026** (behaviour-neutral on the Balanced default): #14 `OffsideTrapController.Update` (v1.2) now consumes `DefensiveSnapshot.OffsideTrapRequested` as the KD-9 **additive request** — a requested trap arms after the reduced `[GT] OffsideTrapRequestedDwellTicks` (≤ baseline `OffsideTrapDwellTicks`) while the four §3.7.2 conditions still adjudicate; default `false` keeps the baseline dwell (byte-identical). #15 `OverloadDetector` (v1.1) gains a 5-arg `Evaluate(…, Flank? preferredFlank, …)` overload (the 4-arg delegates with `null`) consuming the `FocusPlay`→`Flank?` translation as a **bias, not a gate**: a preference for the ball-side flank lowers the overload trigger count by `[GT] OverloadFocusCountBias`; `null` (Mixed/ThroughMiddle) or a ball on the non-preferred flank leaves it unchanged; `AttackingAITick` threads `TacticTranslation.PreferredFlank(snapshot.FocusPlay)`. Both new `[GT]` magnitudes are illustrative pending the §5.6/G2 balance pass. New tests: `DefensiveAITests.T_DA_032`, `AttackingAITests.Evaluate_FocusBias_*`. **The §3.3 per-agent PlayerTactic utility product seam landed June 29, 2026** (behaviour-neutral; `decision-tree/TacticTranslation.cs` v1.2 + `TacticalContext.cs` v1.3 + `UtilityScorer.cs` v1.6 + `MatchEngine.cs` v1.21): `TacticTranslation.PlayerTacticActionMultiplier(in PlayerTactic, Tempo, ActionType)` composes the §3.3 product (`RoleWeightModifiers` × dutyBias × instrBias × `TempoActionBias`) from the existing `TacticalInstructionsConstants` tables; `TacticalContext` gains `Tempo` + per-agent `PlayerTactic` routing fields (Stage0Default seeds `Tempo.Standard` / identity `PlayerTactic.Default(PlayerRole.Default)`); `UtilityScorer.ComputeUtility` applies the product per scored option next to the Mentality multiplier. Identity tactic at Standard ⇒ exactly ×1.0 (FR-TI-031), so a default match is byte-identical; magnitudes illustrative pending §5.6/G2. `RunMechanicsAI` routes the active team `Tempo` (Balanced ⇒ Standard); the **per-agent `PlayerTactic` stays the Stage0Default identity — there is no per-agent tactic config surface at Stage 0** (it lands with the G2 balance pass). `decision-tree/Tests/TacticTranslationTests.cs` extended (identity ⇒ ×1.0 for every action + role/tempo/instr/duty shapes + F5 clamp). **The on-disk tactic-file loader landed June 29, 2026** (`src/match-engine/TeamTacticFileLoader.cs`): `Parse(text) → TeamTacticConfig` — a line-oriented case-insensitive `key = value` grammar under `[home]`/`[away]` headers with `#` comments; every `TeamTactic` field has a key, an omitted key inherits the Balanced identity (an empty/comment-only file ⇒ `TeamTacticConfig.Default` ⇒ behaviour-neutral), unknown key/section + unparsable value + duplicate key + out-of-range `TimeWasting` all throw `FormatException` (fail loud). This is the Stage-0 human-authoring text format (NOT a determinism-pinned wire format — only the resulting `TeamTactic` VALUES enter the digest via v9, never the grammar); it is the parser swap `TeamTacticConfig`/`Applier` were authored to receive, and the Stage-1 `[GT]` loader (FR-CS-019) may replace the grammar leaving `Apply` untouched. `TeamTacticConfig`/`Applier` remarks updated; new `tests/TeamTacticFileLoaderTests.cs`. **ERR-021-002 resolved June 29, 2026** (`SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` 8 → 9; `MatchEngine.cs` v1.22 + `MatchEngineConstants.cs` v1.16): `SerializeWorldState` now serializes both the active AND pending per-team `TeamTactic` (×`TEAM_COUNT`, Appendix B field order) via a new `WriteTeamTactic`, so a **mid-match** tactic change is now restore-deterministic. The per-agent `PlayerTactic` / team `Tempo` in `TacticalContext` need no field — re-assembled each AI tick from the serialized team tactic + the boot identity. New `MatchEngineSnapshotSchemaTests.TeamTactic_FeedsSnapshotDigest` probe (pin 8 → 9). The §3.4 `Clamp01(DefensiveLine + MentalityLineBias)` depth recompute (#12/#14 depth-ownership) remains separately deferred. NOT yet done (Stage-1): the **per-agent tactic config surface** (all agents are the identity `PlayerTactic` at Stage 0) + the **G2 balance pass** pinning the illustrative §3.2/§3.3/§3.4 `[GT]` magnitudes. **The TeamTactic config-loader in-code source + boot applier landed June 29, 2026** (the runtime-activation gate that populates `SetTeamTactic`): new `src/match-engine/TeamTacticConfig.cs` (immutable per-team `TeamTactic`, index = teamId; `Default` = Balanced for every team = behaviour-neutral identity, FR-TI-031; `ForTeam(teamId)` bounds-guarded) + `src/match-engine/TeamTacticConfigApplier.cs` (static `Apply(engine, config)` stages each team's tactic via the public `SetTeamTactic` before kickoff — committed at the first AI-stride boundary per FR-TI-027 — with null-guards). Scope decision: the **on-disk file format is NOT built here** — the `[GT]` config-loader (FR-CS-019) is a Stage 1 deliverable and on-disk encodings are D1-pinned at Stage 0+1, so per the #19 `ScenarioIndex` precedent Stage 0 authors the config in code and the Stage 0+1 disk loader is a pure parser swap that produces a `TeamTacticConfig` and feeds `Apply` unchanged (no invented format, no phantom interface). No production `MatchEngine.cs` change (the applier rides the existing public `SetTeamTactic`). New `src/match-engine/tests/TeamTacticConfigTests.cs` (Default-Balanced; per-team `ForTeam` + bounds throw; applier null-guards; `Apply` routes Attacking/Defending per team through the stride boundary; applying `Default` is digest-identical to the unconfigured run). **The three Stage-1 leftovers landed June 30, 2026** (`MatchEngine.cs` v1.23 + `MatchEngineConstants.cs` v1.17), closing the #21 Stage-1 implementation: (1) **Per-agent tactic config surface** — new per-agent `_active`/`_pendingPlayerTactics[SQUAD_SIZE]` (default identity), public `MatchEngine.SetPlayerTactic(agentId, tactic)` (stages pending; committed at the AI-stride boundary per FR-TI-027), `RunMechanicsAI` routes the active per-agent tactic into `ctx.PlayerTactic` (the §3.3 product seam was already waiting), and new in-code `PlayerTacticConfig` / `PlayerTacticConfigApplier` (mirrors `TeamTacticConfig`; `Identity` = behaviour-neutral; the Stage-0+1 disk loader is a parser swap). Serialized active+pending ×`SQUAD_SIZE` via a new `WritePlayerTactic`; **`SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION` 9 → 10** (a mid-match per-agent change is restore-deterministic — same reasoning as the v9 team tactic). New `TestOnly_PlayerTactic` seam + `MatchEngineTacticTests` / `PlayerTacticConfigTests` / `MatchEngineSnapshotSchemaTests.PlayerTactic_FeedsSnapshotDigest`. (2) **§5.6 / G2 balance pass** — the §3.2/§3.3/§3.4 Mentality/role/duty/tempo/instr `[GT]` magnitudes are **pinned** in `TacticalInstructionsConstants.cs` (values unchanged — already spec-aligned + monotonic, so pinning kept them and added the invariant locks); the catalogue header/class doc + consumer docs (`TacticTranslation` / `UtilityScorer`) reframed illustrative → pinned; spec §3.2/§5.6 record the pass DONE; new `BalancePassInvariantsTests` locks identity-row exactness, strict monotonicity of the risk/line/width/engagement tables, and `RoleWeightModifiers` ∈ [0.5,2.0] with the §3.3 directional shapes. (The #14 `OffsideTrapRequestedDwellTicks` / #15 `OverloadFocusCountBias` activation magnitudes stay tracked under their own consumers.) (3) **§3.4 DefensiveLine depth recompute** — `FillDefensiveSnapshot.DefensiveLineDepth = Clamp01(TeamTactic.DefensiveLine + MentalityLineBias[mentality])`, the manager dial + per-mentality bias as the single depth source feeding #14 (whose `OffensiveLineDepth` output still reaches #8 — `ctx.DefensiveLineDepth` unchanged path). Default Balanced ⇒ Clamp01(0.5 + 0.0) = 0.5 = the prior `STAGE0_DEFENSIVE_LINE_DEPTH`, byte-identical (FR-TI-031). **#21 Stage-1 leftovers are now closed**; the only remaining #21-adjacent work is the Stage-1 `[GT]` disk loader (FR-CS-019), owned outside #21. **The per-agent on-disk tactic-file loader landed June 30, 2026** (`src/match-engine/PlayerTacticFileLoader.cs`): `Parse(text) → PlayerTacticConfig` — the per-agent sibling of `TeamTacticFileLoader`, a line-oriented case-insensitive `key = value` grammar under `[agent N]` headers (N = roster index 0..SQUAD_SIZE−1) with `#` comments; every `PlayerTactic`/`PlayerInstructions` field has a key (`role`/`duty`/`riskyPasses`/`shootTendency`/`dribbleTendency`/`crossTendency`/`positioningFreedom`/`closeDown`/`tightMarking`/`markTarget`/`setPieceRoles`), an omitted key inherits the `PlayerTactic.Default(PlayerRole.Default)` identity and an omitted/empty agent section is the full identity tactic, so an empty/comment-only/`null` file ⇒ `PlayerTacticConfig.Identity` ⇒ behaviour-neutral (FR-TI-031). Unknown key/section, out-of-range or non-numeric agent index, unparsable value, duplicate key, and duplicate section all throw `FormatException` (fail loud). Same scope contract as the team loader — the Stage-0 human-authoring text format, NOT a determinism-pinned wire format (only the resulting `PlayerTactic` VALUES enter the v10 digest, never the grammar); it is the parser swap `PlayerTacticConfig`/`Applier` were authored to receive, and the Stage-1 `[GT]` loader may replace the grammar leaving `Apply` untouched. New `tests/PlayerTacticFileLoaderTests.cs`. The team-side and per-agent in-code config sources + boot appliers + text loaders now all exist; only the pinned `[GT]` disk-encoding swap (FR-CS-019, Stage-1, outside #21) is genuinely outstanding. (dotnet gate not runnable in the authoring environment; compile + suite verified in CI on push.)

- **Dotnet CI gate quarantine burn-down — RESOLVED** — *closed June 13, 2026 (opened June 12, 2026).* All 30 quarantined tests from the first-ever full suite execution have been adjudicated and removed; `tools/dotnet-ci/known-failures.txt` is now empty and the gate (`tools/dotnet-ci/run-gate.sh`; CI job `dotnet-compile-test`) enforces the full 1,165-test suite — any new failure or compile error fails CI. Outcome: **9 production-defects** + at least one spec-defect, remainder test/fixture-defects. The two load-bearing production bugs the never-compiled suites had masked: Shot Mechanics computed sidespin on Unity's `Vector3.up` (+Y = the touchline axis in this Z-up project, §1.2) so curl was identically zero (`ShotSpinCalculator.cs` v1.3); and Perception's `[DERIVED]` `BASE_FOV_HALF_ANGLE` / `PERIPHERAL_ARC_INNER_BOUND` were `static readonly` fields initialised in region order BEFORE their `[GT]` source, so they read 0 at runtime for every consumer (`PerceptionConstants.cs` v1.2, now expression-bodied properties — sibling of the June-12 EventRegistry static-init finding). Other production fixes: Defensive AI ZONAL fallback bypassing the §3.3.3 hysteresis gate; Shot run-up deadband → §3.7.3 linear ramp; Heading synthetic-jump apex frame-quantization (time-warped parabola); Positioning AI §3.5 phase-baseline cancellation (also a spec-defect — `positioning-ai/section-3.md` v0.5) + GK composed-slot NaN guard. Test/fixture-defect clusters: First Touch §5 CQ matrix drift + touch-radius probe construction (4); Pass Mechanics §5 velocity/error/angle bands never re-derived from §3 (3); the 4 singletons (Ball crossbar fixture used post geometry; Collision agents on cell boundaries; Pressing cover-shadow inverted skill term; DetSim replay fixture called recording-side `Encode()`). Proposed spec-error-log entries not yet filed: ERR-005-001 (Pass §5), ERR-012-003..006 (Positioning). Resolutions landed across 8 per-spec commits on `claude/zealous-goldberg-lyrm56`.

- **`Possession.ControlHeight` ↔ `GroundControlHeight` cross-spec routing — RESOLVED** — *closed June 8, 2026 (opened June 3, 2026).* Routing landed: Ball Physics #1 §3.1.11 `BallPhysicsConstants.Possession.ControlHeight` is the **authority**; `FirstTouchConstants.GroundControlHeight` is now a single-consumer `[CROSS]` mirror initialised from `BallPhysicsConstants.Possession.ControlHeight` per Spec #20 §4.2. Rationale: `ControlHeight` is a physical possession-geometry constant living next to the three sibling thresholds (`ControlRadius` / `ControlVelocity` / `ChallengeRadius`); First Touch §3.4.3 consumes it as a routing guard, not as a tunable First-Touch-specific knob. Changes: (a) `src/first-touch/FirstTouchConstants.cs` v1.7 — `GroundControlHeight` moved from `#region GT` to `#region Cross`, retagged `[CROSS]`, RHS now `BallPhysicsConstants.Possession.ControlHeight`; (b) `src/ball-physics/BallPhysicsConstants.cs` v1.7 — XML drift warning on `Possession.ControlHeight` replaced with an AUTHORITY / consumer pointer naming the mirror; (c) `docs/specs/first-touch/section-4-4-to-4-6.md` §3.4.3 constant block — declaration retagged `[CROSS]` and converted from `public const float GROUND_CONTROL_HEIGHT` to `public static readonly float GroundControlHeight = BallPhysicsConstants.Possession.ControlHeight` to match the implementation. **Sibling-hazard sweep cleared:** grep across `docs/specs/` confirms no other spec re-declares `Possession.ControlRadius` / `ControlVelocity` / `ChallengeRadius` — those three remain single-spec `[GT]` in Ball Physics #1 §3.1.11 with no parallel decls; no routing patch needed. **Heading Mechanics #10 follow-up:** Spec #10 references the aerial threshold conceptually but does not declare a parallel constant in `HeadingMechanicsConstants.cs` — when #10's eligibility gate begins consuming the value at Stage 0+1, it MUST mirror from `BallPhysicsConstants.Possession.ControlHeight` for the same reason (not re-declare locally). No silent designer drift remains between possession and first-touch acceptance gates.

Findings: **3 H + 11 M + 9 L**, all resolved in the same commit except ERR-008-006 (documented-open: §3.1.3.4 WIDE_ZONE cross gate undeclarable at Stage 0). Headlines: (H-1) the decision-tree assembly had NEVER compiled — static calls to the instance methods `PassExecutor.Execute`/`ShotExecutor.Execute` + a missing `TacticalDirector.DeterministicSim` asmdef reference — the sixth consecutive spec with a structurally dead build surface and the first where the PRODUCTION assembly (not just tests) was dead; (H-2/ERR-008-002) away-team zone modifiers inverted (shared home-perspective `MatchContext.BallZone` consumed for both teams; §3.2.1.3 zones are "from own goal line"); (H-3) the §3.7.2 state machine was unimplemented (per-tick re-evaluation mid-pass-windup; no forced-refresh suppression; HOLD never reached IDLE). Root cause of the three home/away asymmetry defects (H-2, M-1 press urgency keyed to the AWAY_TEAM literal, M-2 line-depth Y-axis/no-sign — the spec's own §3.4.5 pseudocode carried the axis bug): **every spec worked example and every AR-1 fixture used the home team.** Ten spec-side defects filed and patched as ERR-008-002..011 (one documented-open). Canonical deliverable: `docs/specs/decision-tree/audit-report.md`; tracking in `spec-error-log.md` v1.26 and `src/CLAUDE.md` v1.64. The DT orchestrator seam is the #21 runtime-activation single-writer (`MatchEngine.SetTeamTactic` → `RunMechanicsAI` overlay). New `src/match-engine/tests/MatchEngineAwayTeamScenarios.cs` registers `away-team-tactic-mirror` (Tier B; owning specs `{2,8,16,19,21}`; path under `SCENARIO_PATH_CROSS_SPEC_PREFIX`): boots a real `MatchEngine`, sets home=defending / away=attacking, ticks 300× (5 s), and locks — through the composed host — that **every away agent carries the away (attacking) tactic, every home agent the home (defending) tactic, and the two partitions are distinct** (the exact composition-level inverse of the H-2/M-1/M-2 "away served the home perspective" root cause), plus away agents in bounds and a two-run determinism digest match. `MatchEngineAwayTeamTests.cs` runs it through `ScenarioRunner.Run` → `Passed`. No production change; reads world state via the existing `TestOnly_Mentality/Pressing/Passing` + `TestOnly_AgentSnapshot` seams.

Remote returns HTTP 403 on tag push (branch-protection). Push after merge to main with privileged credentials. **Precondition check (run BEFORE `git push --tags`):** (1) After merge to main, run `git tag -v <tagname>` for each of the 7 tags and confirm the SHA each tag points to is still reachable from `origin/main` (`git merge-base --is-ancestor <tag-sha> origin/main`). (2) If main uses **squash-merge** or **rebase-merge**, the original branch commit SHAs are orphaned and the tags point to dead commits — in that case **delete and recreate** each tag against the corresponding squashed/rebased commit on main (use `git log --grep` or commit-message search to identify the new commit), do NOT just `--force` push. (3) If main uses true merge commits, normal `git push origin --tags` is correct. Document which path was taken in this OPEN ISSUES entry when resolving.
